Module-level diagnostics further show that APG attains 77.5% recall at 25% intersection-over-union, while GRPO-trained VPAR achieves 72.1% selection accuracy on APG-covered queries, compared with 63.4% under supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [95] [PEAK: Precise and Persistent Concept Erasure via k-Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10985)
*Man Jiang,Ouxiang Li,Weibao Xue,Zhenhua Tang,Yuan Wang,Shuo Wang,Yanbin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PEAK是一个基于k-Sparse自编码器（kSAEs）的概念擦除框架，通过将扩散模型内部激活分解为可解释的稀疏特征，精确定位目标概念相关特征并选择性抑制，同时将擦除嵌入模型参数中，实现了精确且持久的文本到图像扩散模型概念擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念擦除方法面临两难困境：一是概念相关表示定位不准确导致意外的语义干扰；二是底层概念知识移除不彻底，使对抗性攻击能够恢复被擦除的概念。因此需要同时实现精确（precise）和持久（persistent）的概念擦除。

Method: PEAK首先在扩散去噪网络的内部激活上训练kSAE，将稠密表示分解为可解释的稀疏特征。通过对比目标提示与非目标提示引发的稀疏激活，根据激活强度和频率识别出一组紧凑的目标特定特征。随后利用这些特征进行参数优化，选择性地抑制目标相关激活，同时保留非目标的互补激活，将概念擦除直接嵌入扩散模型参数中。

Result: 在I2P基准上，PEAK将NudeNet检测从582降至6，平均攻击成功率（ASR）从96.52%降至5.63%，并在MS-COCO上保持了接近零KID的通用生成质量。

Conclusion: PEAK通过特征引导的参数优化实现了无需推理时干预的概念擦除，既保证了擦除的精确性，又通过参数级嵌入实现了对对抗性攻击的有效持久性，在擦除效果和生成质量之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Erasing concepts from large-scale text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models has become increasingly crucial due to the growing concerns over copyright infringement, privacy violations, and offensive content. Existing approaches struggle to achieve both precise and persistent concept erasure: inaccurate localization of concept-related representations may cause unintended semantic interference, while incomplete removal of the underlying concept knowledge allows adversarial recovery. To address this dilemma, we propose PEAK, a \textbf{\textit{precise}} and \textbf{\textit{persistent}} concept erasure framework via k-Sparse Autoencoders (kSAEs). PEAK first trains a kSAE on internal activations of the diffusion denoising network to decompose dense representations into interpretable sparse features. By contrasting sparse activations induced by target and non-target prompts, PEAK identifies a compact set of target-specific features according to both activation strength and frequency. These localized features are then used for parameter optimization, where PEAK selectively suppresses target-related activations while preserving complementary non-target ones towards the original model. This feature-guided optimization embeds concept erasure directly into diffusion parameters, eliminating the need for additional inference-time intervention and facilitating effective persistence against adversarial attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PEAK achieves effective and robust concept erasure. On the I2P benchmark, PEAK reduces NudeNet detections from 582 to 6, lowers the average attack success rate (ASR) from 96.52\% to 5.63\%, and preserves general generation quality on MS-COCO with a near-zero KID. Our code and models are available at: https://github.com/manmanTAT/PEAK

</details>


### [96] [Putting Registers to Work: Task Registers for Token Pruning in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10989)
*Hongsen Cao,Mona Jaber,Shanxin Yuan,Ahmed Sayed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉Transformer中token剪枝策略在不同任务（图像分类、语义分割、目标检测）间的可迁移性问题，发现不同任务对剪枝标准的偏好存在显著差异，并据此提出任务自适应剪枝（TAP）方法，通过引入任务注册token实现跨任务的token剪枝，在保持分类性能的同时大幅提升密集任务的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的token剪枝策略通常针对单一视觉识别任务设计，但预训练的Vision Transformer通常会被复用于多种空间需求不同的下游任务。作者希望探究剪枝策略中的哪些部分可以在图像分类、语义分割和目标检测之间迁移，从而为多任务场景下的高效推理提供指导。

Method: 首先通过受控实验（冻结无剪枝检查点，在单个可剪枝层应用一系列无参数剪枝标准且不做重训练）来探测不同任务的剪枝偏好差异。在此基础上提出TAP方法：区别于现有方法使用共享的注册token，TAP为每个任务引入一个独立的注册token并只激活当前任务对应的token。该token的演化状态负责对token进行排序、在深度上分配精确的移除预算、并设置密集特征的恢复尺度。进一步提出TAP-J变体，对模型进行联合适应训练。

Result: 三个关键发现：（1）分割和检测任务对剪枝标准的排序与分类不同；（2）分类任务在最早层对基于注意力的剪枝尤为敏感；（3）密集任务偏好相反的恢复端点。在最终保持率ρ=0.5时，TAP-J在ADE20K上达到47.0 mIoU且编码器吞吐量提升1.30倍，在COCO上达到53.7 box AP且编码器吞吐量提升1.32倍，同时在ImageNet-1K上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 剪枝策略在不同视觉任务间的迁移具有任务特异性，统一的剪枝策略难以适用于所有任务。TAP通过任务自适应设计成功地在保持分类性能的同时大幅加速了密集预测任务，证明了任务感知的token剪枝设计的有效性。

Abstract: Token-pruning policies are usually designed for a single recognition pipeline, but pretrained Vision Transformers are reused across tasks with different spatial demands. We ask which parts of a pruning policy transfer across image classification, semantic segmentation, and object detection. For each pipeline, controlled probes freeze the no-pruning checkpoint and apply a series of parameter-free reduction criteria at one eligible layer at a time without retraining. The probes reveal three differences: segmentation and detection rank the criteria differently, classification is especially sensitive to attention-based pruning in the earliest layers, and the dense tasks prefer opposite recovery endpoints. These findings motivate Task-Adaptive Pruning (TAP). Existing register tokens serve as task-agnostic storage for feature artifacts. TAP instead introduces one task register per task and activates only the current one. Its evolving state ranks tokens, distributes an exact removal budget over depth, and sets the recovery scale for dense features. At a final keep rate of $ρ=0.5$, our jointly adapted model, TAP-J, reaches $47.0$ mIoU at $1.30\times$ encoder throughput on ADE20K and $53.7$ box AP at $1.32\times$ encoder throughput on COCO while remaining competitive on ImageNet-1K.

</details>


### [97] [HNDiff: Haze-Noise Diffusion for Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10995)
*Jin-Ting He,Fu-Jen Tsai,Yan-Tsung Peng,Min-Hung Chen,Chia-Wen Lin,Yen-Yu Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Haze-Noise Diffusion (HNDiff)框架，将大气散射模型嵌入扩散模型作为归纳偏置，通过联合雾霾-噪声扩散调度器实现物理一致的图像去雾，并扩展出Latent HNDiff提升现有去雾网络性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的图像去雾方法忽视了雾霾形成的物理过程，从纯高斯噪声重建干净图像，限制了恢复潜力。需要将物理先验融入扩散框架以更好地对齐雾霾形成机制。

Method: 1) 将大气散射模型作为归纳偏置嵌入扩散框架；2) 前向过程引入联合雾-噪扩散，使用雾感知噪声调度器，根据雾密度自适应调整噪声注入强度；3) 反向过程推导物理一致的去雾-去噪过程，同步去除雾霾和噪声；4) 提出Latent HNDiff，编译干净潜空间先验以增强现有去雾网络。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法显著提升主流去雾骨干网络性能，在多个基准数据集上达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 将物理模型嵌入扩散过程能更好地对齐真实雾霾形成机制，且潜空间扩展可增强现有去雾网络的实用性。

Abstract: Existing diffusion-based methods have recently made significant progress in image dehazing. However, they typically neglect the physics of haze formation and reconstruct clean images from pure Gaussian noise, thereby limiting their restoration potential. To address this issue, we propose Haze-Noise Diffusion (HNDiff), a novel diffusion framework that embeds the atmospheric scattering model as an inductive bias. By grounding diffusion in physical principles, HNDiff ensures that the restoration aligns more closely with underlying mechanisms of haze formation. In its forward process, we introduce joint haze-noise diffusion with a haze-aware noise scheduler, which progressively adds both haze and noise to an image. Essentially, the scheduler adapts noise levels according to haze density, meaning that regions with heavier haze receive stronger noise injection to encourage content generation, while clearer regions receive lighter noise to better preserve details, which directly links the forward degradation process with the physics of haze. In the reverse process, we then derive a physically consistent dehazing-denoising process that simultaneously removes haze and noise to restore a clean image in a manner aligned with the forward degradation process. To further enhance practicality, we propose Latent HNDiff, which compiles clean latent priors that can be seamlessly integrated into existing dehazing networks to boost performance. Extensive experiments show that our work significantly improves leading dehazing backbones and achieves state-of-the-art results on benchmark datasets. The project page is available at https://jin-ting-he.github.io/HNDiff .

</details>


### [98] [Watching Synthetic Videos: Aligning Cross-modal Representations with Visual Synthesis for Zero-shot Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11013)
*Liangyu Fu,Junbo Wang,Yuke Li,Ya Jing,Xuecheng Wu,Zhiyong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型零样本视频字幕生成框架（WSV），利用预训练文生视频模型生成合成视频潜在表征，并通过polisher和prompter两个模块弥合文本与视频之间的跨模态差距，在MSVD、MSR-VTT和VATEX数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本视频字幕生成中，纯文本训练范式导致训练（文本）与推理（视频）之间存在跨模态差距，以往的简单线性变换方法不足以实现充分的多模态表示空间对齐，生成的字幕准确度不高。

Method: 提出WSV框架，包含两个训练阶段：第一阶段利用预训练文生视频模型生成合成视频潜在表征，并设计一个polisher模块弥合真实与合成视频分布之间的差距；第二阶段设计prompter模块，以精炼后的潜在表征为条件输入GPT-2生成字幕。推理时，输入视频通过预训练3D Causal VAE编码后直接送入prompter引导GPT-2生成最终字幕。

Result: 在MSVD、MSR-VTT和VATEX三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在B@4和CIDEr指标上分别达到52和95.7的分数。

Conclusion: 通过引入文生视频模型生成合成视频潜在表征，并设计专门的polisher和prompter模块，有效缩小了文本与视频之间的跨模态差距，显著提升了零样本视频字幕生成的准确性。

Abstract: Text-only training is a popular paradigm in zero-shot video captioning, where the video distribution is not available to the model during training, leading to a cross-modal gap between the training (text-only) and the inference (video-only). Previous works attempt to bridge the gap through simple linear transformations. However, the inherent gap between text and video makes cross-modal representation space alignment insufficient, resulting in inaccurate sentences. To address this issue, we propose a novel zero-shot video captioning framework (WSV) consisting of two training stages, which first generates corresponding synthetic video latent representations via a pretrained text-to-video generation model. To strengthen the fidelity of the latent representations, we propose a polisher capable of bridging the gap between real and synthetic video distributions. Subsequently, we design a prompter that conditions GPT-2 on the polished latent representations to generate the captions in the second training stage. During inference, an input video is encoded by a pretrained 3D Causal VAE and then fed directly into the prompter, which in turn guides GPT-2 to produce the final caption. Experimental results conducted on MSVD, MSR-VTT, and VATEX datasets demonstrate that our proposed method achieves scores of 52 and 95.7 on the B@4 and CIDEr metrics, respectively.

</details>


### [99] [When Visual Signals Mislead: A Mechanistic Study of Attribute Hallucination in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11024)
*Yufei Zhang,Chenlu Zhan,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISOR框架，通过空图像诊断（VSNR）将VLMs的预测分解为视觉信号和语言先验信号，发现属性幻觉主要由视觉信号而非语言先验主导，并据此采用校准、弃权或视觉适配等路由式修复策略，在三个VLM家族上有效降低属性误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性幻觉在视觉-语言模型中普遍存在，但主流解释"语言先验主导"从未在属性层面被直接验证，导致基于该假设的抑制方法可能建立在错误前提上。

Method: 提出VISOR统一框架，包含VSNR诊断（将预测分解为视觉logit信号和语言先验信号）和路由式修复（根据失败模式分流到校准、弃权或针对性视觉适配三种算子）。

Result: 在10,791个负样本、三个VLM家族和三类属性上，视觉信号强烈预测假阳性而语言先验信号接近随机；VISOR在Qwen、InternVL和LLaVA上均降低了属性误报。

Conclusion: 属性幻觉的主因是视觉信号问题而非语言先验主导，VISOR证明无需依赖先验主导假设即可有效修复属性幻觉。

Abstract: Attribute hallucination---where vision-language models (VLMs) correctly identify an object but mischaracterize its properties---is prevalent yet mechanistically poorly understood. The dominant explanation, language-prior dominance, has motivated prior-suppression methods, but this explanation has not been directly tested at the attribute level. We present VISOR (Visual-Operational Remediation), a unified framework that couples null-image-based diagnosis with routed remediation. Its VSNR diagnostic decomposes each prediction into a visual logit signal and a language-prior signal. Across 10,791 negative-ground-truth samples from three VLM families and three attribute types, the visual signal strongly predicts false positives, whereas the language-prior signal is near chance. VISOR uses this diagnosis to separate two failure modes: low-margin but directionally correct visual signals in color/state attributes, and low-SNR or misaligned visual signals in material attributes. The same diagnosis routes each query to the appropriate operator: calibration for threshold-placement errors, abstention for training-free low-SNR handling, or targeted visual adaptation for material failures that prior suppression cannot correct. Across Qwen, InternVL, and LLaVA, VISOR reduces attribute false positives without relying on the prior-dominance assumption.

</details>


### [100] [Multi-Level Evidence Aggregation for Robust Facial Phenotype Retrieval in Rare Genetic Disorder Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11037)
*Alexander Hustinx,Carolin Kaffiné,Behnam Javanmardi,Tzung-Chien Hsieh,Peter Krawitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种推理时的多层级证据聚合框架，在不修改底层GestaltMatcher-Arc编码器的情况下，通过结合患者多图像嵌入聚合、患者加权疾病质心和混合个体-质心评分，显著提升了面部表型检索在罕见遗传病优先级排序中的准确率（GMDB-Freq top-1从38.52%提升至48.82%，GMDB-Rare从19.38%提升至23.79%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GestaltMatcher检索框架采用逐点比较方式，将每个测试图像与图库中单个图像进行逐一比对，未能充分利用患者的多个图像以及同一疾病由多个已确诊患者表示的证据信息，导致检索效果受限。

Method: 提出推理时多层级证据聚合框架，主要包括三个层次：(1) 嵌入层面对同一患者的多个图像进行患者级聚合；(2) 构建患者加权的疾病质心表示；(3) 采用混合个体-质心评分策略，整合测试患者观测、疾病级图库证据和局部最近邻证据。

Result: 在GMDB v1.1.4上，多层级证据聚合在所有评估子集上均提升了平均每疾病top-N检索准确率。GMDB-Freq上top-1准确率从38.52%提升至48.82%，GMDB-Rare上从19.38%提升至23.79%；多图像子集上top-1从46.12%提升至60.94%（GMDB-Multi-Freq），从18.54%提升至26.71%（GMDB-Multi-Rare）。

Conclusion: 推理时聚合无需重新训练编码器即可提升下一代面部表型检索性能，支持从孤立的单图像匹配向患者和疾病证据的多层级聚合转变，有助于罕见遗传疾病的优先级排序。

Abstract: AI-assisted facial phenotyping supports rare genetic disorder prioritization by retrieving visually similar diagnosed cases from facial image reference databases such as the GestaltMatcher Database (GMDB). Existing GestaltMatcher-based retrieval frameworks compare each test image with individual gallery images in a facial phenotype embedding space. However, this pointwise formulation does not fully exploit available evidence, because patients may have multiple images and disorders may be represented by multiple diagnosed gallery patients.
  We propose an inference-time multi-level evidence aggregation framework that improves facial phenotype retrieval without modifying the underlying GestaltMatcher-Arc encoder. The framework combines embedding-level patient aggregation of multiple images from the same individual, patient-weighted disorder centroids, and hybrid individual-centroid scoring to integrate test-patient observations, disorder-level gallery evidence, and local nearest-neighbor evidence.
  We evaluated the approach on GMDB v1.1.4 across disorders represented during training (GMDB-Freq), unseen disorders (GMDB-Rare), and multi-image patient subsets, using a unified gallery containing both GMDB-Freq and GMDB-Rare disorders. Multi-level evidence aggregation improved mean per-disorder top-$N$ retrieval accuracy across all evaluation subsets. Top-1 accuracy increased from 38.52% to 48.82% on GMDB-Freq and from 19.38% to 23.79% on GMDB-Rare. On multi-image subsets, top-1 accuracy increased from 46.12% to 60.94% on GMDB-Multi-Freq and from 18.54% to 26.71% on GMDB-Multi-Rare.
  These findings show that inference-time aggregation can improve next-generation facial phenotype retrieval without retraining the encoder, supporting a shift from isolated single-image matching toward multi-level aggregation of patient and disorder evidence for rare-disorder prioritization.

</details>


### [101] [3D Weighted Geometric Graph Neural Networks for Sheep Facial Pain Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11050)
*Alam Noor,Luis Almeida,Mohamed Daoudi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-SPFES系统，一种基于单目深度感知的加权几何图神经网络，将绵羊疼痛面部表情量表（SPFES）的面部关键点映射到3D欧几里得空间中，通过VideoDepthAnything从单张RGB图像估计深度，无需专用深度硬件，最终输出0-100%的标准化疼痛评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习系统主要在2D单图像域中处理面部，将面部视为单维度表示，忽略了绵羊面部的3D解剖结构以及跨关键点之间的空间关系，而这些关系是临床验证的SPFES所固有的。

Method: 系统包含：(1) 使用VideoDepthAnything从单目RGB相机估计3D坐标；(2) 每个关键点节点包含3D空间坐标、表面法线和面部属性类别嵌入的特征向量；(3) 边权重基于欧氏距离与表面共面性的聚合度量；(4) 加权几何图神经网络（WG-GNN）采用K=3层几何感知消息传递，结合缩放点积注意力机制增强解剖相关关键点间的消息；(5) 输出O=3个疼痛水平聚类并整合为置信度加权的标准化疼痛评分（NPS）。

Result: 该系统实现了从单张RGB图像到3D疼痛面部表情分析的全流程，能够输出0-100%范围内的标准化疼痛评分，且无需专业深度传感器硬件。

Conclusion: 3D-SPFES提供了一种新颖的疼痛评估方法，通过将SPFES面部关键点整合到3D图神经网络中，更好地利用了跨关键点的空间关系，从而提升绵羊疼痛评估的准确性和临床适用性。

Abstract: Deep learning systems perform mainly within the 2D for a single image domain and take the face as a single-dimension representation, losing sight of the 3D anatomy of sheep and cross-landmark spatial relationships that are intrinsic to the clinically proven Sheep Pain Facial Expression Scale (SPFES). This paper presents the \textbf{3D Sheep Pain Facial Expression System (3D-SPFES)}, a novel, monocular depth-aware geometric graph neural network system that integrates each SPFES facial landmark, such as the ears, eyes, and nose, into 3D Euclidean space estimated from a single RGB camera by using VideoDepthAnything, thus preventing the need for specialized depth hardware. Each landmark node includes a feature vector containing its 3D spatial coordinates, estimated surface normal, and facial attribute class embedding. Edges linked to nodes are assigned weights based on an aggregate metric that combines both Euclidean distance and surface co-planarity in a 3D space. A Weighted Geometric Graph Neural Network (WG-GNN) studies this graph using $\mathcal{K} = 3$ geometry-aware message-passing layers enhanced by a scaled dot-product attention method that selectively enhances anatomically relevant inter-landmark messages. The resultant node embeddings are combined into $\mathcal{O} = 3$ pain-level clusters and integrated into a Normalized Pain Score (NPS) within the range of $[0, 100%]$ a confidence-weighted, SPFES-derived scoring method.

</details>


### [102] [HUI360: A 360° Egocentric Dataset and Baselines for Human-Robot Interaction Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11051)
*Raphael Lorenzo-Louis,Fabio Amadio,Bertrand Luvison,Serena Ivaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HUI360，这是目前最大的野外人机交互（HRI）预期数据集。数据集由移动机器人在三个月内跨多个环境和日期自然采集，包含360度全景影像、100万个预处理标注（2D姿态、面部关键点、分割掩码）、手动精炼的交互标注，以及首个跨数据集交互预期的基准评估。同时发布了SSUP-HRI数据集的600万标注用于跨数据集评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人在人类环境中日益普及，自动预期人机交互对于实现主动和社交感知行为至关重要。然而，现有数据集在规模、环境多样性和自然性方面存在不足，限制了交互预期模型的泛化能力评估与提升。

Method: 设计了一套自动交互标注流程和代码，适用于任意360度等距柱状投影视频；结合手动精炼接口保证标注质量；利用最先进的计算机视觉方法生成2D姿态、面部关键点和分割掩码；建立基准基线并引入首个跨数据集评估方案，同时发布SSUP-HRI数据集的额外600万标注。

Result: 发布了HUI360数据集，包含100万个预处理标注和原始全景360度图像（按需提供，遵守GDPR），建立了包括首个跨数据集评估在内的交互预期基准基线，并额外发布600万SSUP-HRI数据集标注。

Conclusion: HUI360作为目前最大的野外人机交互预期数据集，通过多样化的自然采集场景和全面的标注，为交互预期模型的泛化能力评估与改进提供了重要资源，推动了该领域的基准研究发展。

Abstract: As robots increasingly operate in human-populated environments, anticipating human intentions is essential for enabling proactive and socially aware behavior. Automatic anticipation of human-robot interactions is thus emerging as a crucial perception challenge for embodied agents. To this end, we introduce HUI360, the largest dataset for human-robot interaction anticipation in the wild and its set of baselines. The dataset was collected from a mobile robot, in the wild, over multiple days within a 3-month period, and in several environments, capturing natural, spontaneous behaviors from both passersby and users, and encompassing a diverse range of individuals. This variety enables evaluating and improving the generalization capabilities of interaction anticipation models. We designed a pipeline and share code for automatic interaction annotation in arbitrary 360-degree equirectangular videos, along with interfaces for manual refinement. Using this pipeline, we release the HUI360 open set of 1M pre-processed annotations, including detailed 2D poses, facial keypoints, and segmentation masks, obtained using state-of-the-art computer vision methods and manually curated to ensure high-quality tracking and interaction annotation. Additionally, we release the raw panoptic 360-degree images captured from the robot's egocentric viewpoint (on demand, for research purpose only in compliance with GDPR). Finally, we establish benchmark baselines for interaction anticipation, including the first cross-dataset evaluations for this task: to this end, we also release 6M annotations for another existing in-the-wild outdoor dataset collected from a mobile robot (SSUP-HRI). Dataset and code can be found at https://hucebot.github.io/hui360.

</details>


### [103] [A Comparative Evaluation of Deep Learning Object Detection Models on a Real-World Multi-Plant Dataset from Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11053)
*Ismail Ismail Tijjani,Sunusi Muhammad Ibrahim,Amina Ibrahim Khaleel,Lanre Olusegun Akinola,Fatima Isa Jibrin,Muhammad Bashir Aliyu,Abdullahi Abdussalam Dalhat,Abdullahi Suiudeen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究使用从尼日利亚农田采集的真实数据集AgriAISeg（3382张图像，涵盖芝麻、卷心菜和番茄作物），对六种目标检测模型（YOLOv5、YOLOv8、YOLO11、YOLO26、Faster R-CNN和RT-DETR）进行了比较评估。结果表明RT-DETR性能最佳（精确率0.768，mAP@0.5:0.95为0.624），YOLOv8和YOLO11表现稳定，而Faster R-CNN在复杂田间条件下效果显著较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉农业应用大多依赖于受控数据集，无法真实反映实际农田条件，尤其缺乏对非洲等欠发达地区的代表性。作者旨在通过真实田间数据集评估不同检测模型在复杂环境下的性能，为实际农业应用提供可靠参考。

Method: 构建了来自尼日利亚农场的真实数据集AgriAISeg，包含3382张芝麻、卷心菜和番茄作物图像，覆盖光照变化、遮挡和视角差异等复杂条件。对YOLOv5、YOLOv8、YOLO11、YOLO26、Faster R-CNN和RT-DETR六种模型进行训练，使用精确率、召回率、mAP@0.5和mAP@0.5:0.95作为评估指标。

Result: RT-DETR表现最优，精确率为0.768，mAP@0.5:0.95为0.624；YOLOv8和YOLO11表现稳定且一致；Faster R-CNN整体mAP@0.5仅为0.466，准确率显著偏低；YOLO系列模型训练效率优于Faster R-CNN。

Conclusion: 现代单阶段检测器和基于Transformer的检测器在真实农业环境中比两阶段方法（如Faster R-CNN）提供了更可靠、更高效的植物检测解决方案，为农业精准化监测中的模型选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: The application of computer vision in agriculture has shown significant potential for improving crop monitoring and precision farming. However, many existing approaches rely on controlled datasets that do not adequately represent realworld farming conditions, particularly in underrepresented regions such as Africa. This study presents a comparative evaluation of six object detection models YOLOv5, YOLOv8, YOLO11, YOLO26, Faster R-CNN, and RT-DETR using a real-world dataset, AgriAISeg 1 , collected manually from Nigerian farms. AgriAISeg comprises 3,382 images of sesame, cabbage, and tomato crops captured under varying environmental conditions, including changes in illumination, occlusion, and viewing perspectives. Models were trained, and performance was assessed using precision, recall, mAP@0.5, and mAP@0.5:0.95. The results show that RT-DETR achieved the highest overall performance with a precision of 0.768 and mAP@0.5:0.95 of 0.624, while YOLOv8 and YOLO11 also demonstrated strong and consistent performance. In contrast, Faster R-CNN recorded significantly lower accuracy, with an overall mAP@0.5 of 0.466, indicating reduced effectiveness under complex field conditions. In addition, YOLO-based models exhibited superior training efficiency compared to Faster R-CNN.These findings demonstrate that modern one-stage and transformer-based detectors provide more reliable and efficient solutions for plant detection in realworld agricultural environments.

</details>


### [104] [Entropy-Centric Explainable AI for Remote Sensing Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11064)
*Ali Saleh,Abdul Karim Gizzini,Mohamad Ghassany,Ali J. Ghandour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以熵为中心的可解释AI（XAI）方法用于语义分割，并设计了一套新的XAI评估方法来衡量高亮区域的相关性，实验证明该方法优于近期适配于语义分割的XAI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在遥感等关键领域，黑盒深度学习模型缺乏透明度限制了用户信任和实际采用。虽然图像分类的XAI研究已取得显著进展，但针对图像分割任务的XAI方法仍有很大的改进空间，因此需要提出更有效的语义分割解释方法。

Method: 提出一种以熵为中心的XAI方法（熵中心方法）用于语义分割任务的决策解释，同时设计了一种新的XAI评估方法论，用于高效衡量所提方法高亮区域的相关性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的熵中心XAI方法在语义分割任务上优于近期适配的其他XAI方法。

Conclusion: 所提的熵中心XAI方法在语义分割中展现出更好的解释性能，配合新的评估方法论，有助于提升关键领域（如遥感）中黑盒模型的透明度和可信度。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) has become a powerful approach to solving complex problems in critical domains. Many concerns arise regarding the decision-making process of its models, mainly due to deep neural networks outperforming their peers at the cost of ambiguity in feature extraction and prediction. Consequently, in critical domains such as remote sensing, where high-resolution imagery must be analyzed using black-box models, the lack of transparency limits trust in these models and, thus, their adoption. In light of this reality, explaining and understanding the complex decision-making process of AI models has become essential. Explainable AI (XAI) aims to bridge this gap by providing insights into how and why certain decisions are made. While significant progress has been achieved in explaining image classification tasks, image segmentation still offers considerable room for improvement. In this context, this paper proposes an entropy-centric XAI method for semantic segmentation. Moreover, a new XAI evaluation methodology is proposed to efficiently measure the relevance of the regions highlighted by the proposed XAI method. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of the proposed XAI method compared with recently adapted XAI methods for semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [105] [CapProbe: Evaluating Detailed Image Captions via Full-Scene Dense Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11074)
*Mouxiao Huang,Qiangyu Yan,Borui Jiang,Han Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CapProbe，一个全场景密集QA基准，将详细图像描述评估转化为区域对齐的事实核查。包含346张图像、1,868个区域和25,650个问题，覆盖37个L1域和219个L2子域，通过语言评判器从标题中回答问题，使用有效准确率和密度指标评估VLM生成描述的全面性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考指标（如CIDEr、SPICE）和LLM评分协议难以验证密集事实声明，而QA类替代方案存在探测密度低、领域覆盖窄、缺乏问题与图像区域显式对齐等问题。

Method: 将每张图像分解为覆盖前景和背景的粗粒度语义区域；为每个区域生成涵盖10个语义类别的多项选择题；基于37个L1域和219个L2子域的两层分类法构建；通过语言评判器仅从标题作答，设置"不确定"选项和有效准确率来区分未回答与错误回答，用密度指标惩罚冗长但信息量低的描述。

Result: 在13个VLM上的实验显示：模型间覆盖率差距大，存在清晰的效能-效率权衡，以及稀疏或重叠度评估常遗漏的失败模式。该协议成本效益高，将无约束标量评分转化为结构化多项选择阅读，减少开放式评分偏差，并在固定阅读器下产生相对稳定的模型排名。

Conclusion: CapProbe提供了一种评判器依赖但成本高效的密集QA评估范式，能发现传统方法遗漏的模型失败模式，未来将公开基准数据、标注和评估代码。

Abstract: Evaluating detailed image captions from Vision-Language Models (VLMs) requires going beyond surface-level semantic similarity. Reference-based metrics (e.g., CIDEr and SPICE) and LLM-as-scorer protocols struggle to verify dense factual claims, while existing QA-based alternatives generally offer lower probe density, narrower domain coverage, or no explicit alignment between individual questions and segmented image regions. We introduce CapProbe, a full-scene dense QA benchmark that turns detailed caption evaluation into region-aligned factual checking. Each image is decomposed into coarse semantic regions covering both foreground and background elements; for every retained region, we generate multiple-choice questions spanning 10 semantic categories, forming a dense checklist of probed visual facts. Guided by a two-tier taxonomy of 37 L1 domains and 219 L2 sub-domains, CapProbe comprises 346 images, 1,868 regions, and 25,650 questions, averaging 74 QA pairs per image. A language judge answers from the caption alone; an Uncertain option and Effective Accuracy provide a judge-dependent proxy for distinguishing unanswered probes from incorrectly resolved ones, while density-based metrics penalize verbose yet uninformative captions. The protocol is cost-effective: by converting unconstrained scalar scoring into structured MCQ reading, it reduces open-ended scoring bias while remaining judge-conditioned and yields relatively stable model rankings under a fixed reader. Experiments on 13 VLMs show large Coverage gaps across models, a clear competency-efficiency trade-off, and failure modes that sparse or overlap-based evaluation often misses. The benchmark data, annotations, and evaluation code will be released soon.

</details>


### [106] [Static in Frames, Dynamic in Events: Rethinking Features in Event Cameras as Motion Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11075)
*Hesam Araghi,Jan van Gemert,Nergis Tomen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析了事件相机角点检测中使用的两个特征（结构张量特征值和时空密度值），证明它们是运动线索，并将这些特征与局部几何信息结合，用于增强事件光流估计任务。通过理论分析、合成数据实验和DSEC基准测试，验证了所提特征能持续提升光流网络精度，尤其在数据稀缺场景和小容量模型上增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机异步捕捉强度变化，与传统帧式视觉不同，事件数据本身编码了场景动态和物体运动信息。然而，现有事件角点检测中使用的特征（结构张量特征值和时空密度值）尚未被系统地分析与利用。作者假设这些特征结合局部几何信息可以增强运动估计任务，因此旨在验证这一假设并加以利用。

Method: 首先从理论上分析运动角点处的结构张量特征值与运动方向之间的关系；然后在合成数据集上设计受控实验，验证将局部几何特征扩展为结合特征值和密度值能提供互补的运动信息且对纹理和散粒噪声鲁棒；最后将所提特征集成到最先进的事件光流网络中，在真实DSEC基准上进行评估。

Result: 在DSEC真实世界基准上，加入所提特征后光流网络精度一致提升，最大增益出现在数据稀缺场景和较低容量模型中，证明了特征的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: 事件角点检测中的结构张量特征值和时空密度值本质上是运动线索，将其与局部几何信息结合可以增强事件光流估计性能，为事件相机下游任务提供了有价值的特征扩展方案。

Abstract: Event cameras capture intensity changes asynchronously with high temporal resolution, requiring novel preprocessing methods for downstream tasks. Unlike static intensity snapshots, event data inherently encode information about scene dynamics and object motion, meaning that features derived from events can exhibit behaviors with no direct analogue in frame-based vision. In this paper, we analyze two features used in event-based corner detection---the eigenvalues of the structure tensor and the spatiotemporal density values---and show that they are \emph{motion cues}. We hypothesize that these features, combined with local geometric information, can enhance motion estimation tasks. To validate this, we first theoretically analyze how the eigenvalues of the structure tensor at moving corner points relate to the direction of motion. We then design controlled experiments on a synthetic dataset, confirming that extending local geometric features with eigenvalues and density values provides complementary motion information and is robust to texture and shot noise. Finally, we integrate the proposed features into a state-of-the-art event-based optical flow network and evaluate on the real-world DSEC benchmark, where the added features consistently improve accuracy, with the largest gains in data-scarce scenarios and for lower-capacity models. The code for this paper can be found at: \href{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}.

</details>


### [107] [Foundation Model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling (FEEDS): A label-efficient training strategy for pan-cancer, multi-tracer PET/CT datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11076)
*Biratal Raj Wagle,Bashirul Azam Biswas,Grant Chau,Matthew E. Maeder,Muhammad Azeem Arshad,Michael S. Leapman,James B. Yu,Indrani Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FEEDS（基础模型驱动的高效数据采样）策略，利用视觉基础模型嵌入从大规模未标注PET/CT数据中选取最具信息量和多样性的病例供专家标注，以70%的标注减少量达到与全量标注（100%）相当的病变分割性能，并在多中心、多示踪剂、多病种数据集上验证了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT中的自动化病变分割有助于癌症检测、分期和治疗规划，但训练高质量分割模型需要大量标注数据，而标注既耗时又需要专业知识。在有限标注数据上训练的模型缺乏临床所需的准确性和泛化能力，因此亟需一种标签高效和计算高效的标注采样策略。

Method: FEEDS是一种一步式训练范式，利用视觉基础模型嵌入来选择最具信息量和多样性的未标注病例供专家标注。与无监督、半监督和主动学习不同，FEEDS只需有限且具有代表性的训练集，无需迭代标注循环。训练和验证基于AutoPET-III数据集，并在AutoPET-III、DeepPSMA和内部达特茅斯-希区柯克医学中心三个留出集上测试。

Result: FEEDS在体素、病灶和解剖区域三个层面优于随机采样标注、伪标签半监督学习及仅用有限标注数据的训练。该方法在三个测试集、FDG与PSMA示踪剂以及多种疾病上均表现出良好的泛化能力，以70%的标注减少量达到与全量标注（100%）训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: FEEDS有效解决了自动病变分割框架中的标签稀缺问题，为从大规模未标注临床数据存储库中构建代表性且多样化的标注队列提供了实用方案，在临床应用中具有显著价值。

Abstract: Automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT imaging can assist clinicians with cancer detection, staging, and treatment planning across radiotracers and cancer types. However, training lesion segmentation models that capture variations in lesion size, distribution, and appearance requires large annotated datasets, whose creation is both time- and expertise-intensive. As a result, models trained on limited labeled PET/CT data often lack the accuracy and generalizability needed for clinical use. We present FEEDS (Foundation model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling), a label- and compute-efficient learning strategy that uses vision foundation model embeddings to select the most informative and diverse unlabeled cases for expert annotation. Unlike unsupervised, semi-supervised, and active learning approaches, FEEDS is a one-step training paradigm requiring only a limited, representative training set, making it label- and compute-efficient. We train and validate FEEDS using the AutoPET-III dataset. We test its accuracy and generalizability on three held-out sets: AutoPET-III, DeepPSMA, and an internal Dartmouth-Hitchcock Medical Center dataset. We evaluate clinical utility at the voxel, lesion, and anatomic region level to assess performance in high-risk areas and treatment planning utility. FEEDS outperforms random-sampling-based labeling, pseudolabel-based semi-supervised learning, and training with limited labeled data alone. It generalizes across all three test sets, FDG and PSMA tracers, and multiple diseases, matching fully-labeled (100\%) training performance with 70\% less annotation burden. FEEDS addresses the challenge of label scarcity in an automatic lesion segmentation framework by providing a practical approach for constructing representative and diverse annotation queues from large, unannotated clinical repositories.

</details>


### [108] [Learning Gaussian Structure: Intervention-Guided Density Control for Feed-Forward Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11077)
*Hang Li,Jiahe Li,Meiying Gu,Jin Zheng,Lina Yu,Xiao Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LGS框架，通过学习高斯密集化策略（Gaussian Densify Policy）和跨时间点查询（Cross-Time Point Query），在推理阶段动态调整高斯结构并增强属性预测，在Waymo和PandaSet数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前馈高斯重建方法（如基于LiDAR的方法）在推理时保持初始的观测点与高斯原语的对应关系，将初始化的原语集合视为最终表示。这存在两个问题：一是无法像优化式3DGS那样在训练中累积梯度来决定场景表示如何密集化；二是共享的稀疏主干只能隐式融合不同时间戳的观测，没有显式聚合单个原语的跨时间证据。

Method: LGS框架包含两个核心组件：（1）高斯密集化策略（Gaussian Densify Policy）——基于关键观察：由剪枝或添加干预引起的局部梯度响应变化可以揭示相应结构调整是否有利于重建，通过学习包含剪枝分数和添加分数的密集化图（Densify Map），在推理阶段直接调整高斯结构；（2）紧凑的跨时间点查询（Cross-Time Point Query）——显式检索并聚合其他时间戳高斯原语的邻近特征，用于更可靠的属性预测。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset和PandaSet上的大量实验表明，LGS在重建性能上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LGS通过同时增强高斯结构（密集化策略）和高斯属性（跨时间聚合），有效解决了前馈LiDAR重建中无法动态调整结构和无法显式融合跨时间证据的问题，验证了干预式学习结构调整策略的有效性。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction has recently emerged as an efficient approach for driving scene reconstruction. However, prevailing LiDAR-based methods preserve the initial correspondence between observed points and Gaussian primitives, treating the initialized primitive set as the final representation. Unlike optimization-based 3DGS, these methods cannot accumulate gradients during training to determine how the scenes representation should be densified. Meanwhile, the shared sparse backbone only fuses observations from different timestamps implicitly, without explicitly aggregating cross-time evidence for individual primitives. In this paper, we present Learning Gaussian Structure (LGS), a framework that enhances both Gaussian structure and primitive attributes. Our key observation is that changes in local gradient responses induced by a prune or add intervention reveal whether the corresponding structural adjustment benefits reconstruction. Based on this observation, our Gaussian Densify Policy learns a Densify Map comprising Prune and Addition Scores from controlled interventions, and directly adjusts the Gaussian structure during inference. We further develop a compact Cross-Time Point Query that explicitly retrieves and aggregates neighboring features from Gaussian primitives at other timestamps for reliable attribute prediction. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset and PandaSet demonstrate that LGS consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [109] [Every Packet Counts: Dispersing Information for Loss-Resilient Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11096)
*Yuhang Wei,Chuqin Zhou,Yibo Shi,Jing Wang,Guo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的抗丢包图像压缩方案，通过通道间重分布（ICR）、交错通道分组（ICG）和双层双分支自回归结构，同时解决包化阶段信息分布不均和熵编码阶段顺序解码依赖两个问题，在20%丢包率下平均PSNR提升1.84 dB，方差降低一个数量级，且能泛化到突发性丢包场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型图像压缩方法在卫星和应急通信等场景中极易受到数据包丢失的影响，该脆弱性源于包化阶段的信息分布不均匀以及熵编码阶段的顺序解码依赖，亟需针对性的解决方案。

Method: 提出了三个关键组件：(1) 通道间重分布（ICR）机制，在包化前重新分配通道能量，防止关键信息集中在少量通道中；(2) 交错通道分组（ICG）策略，以跨步方式划分潜在通道，将信息分散到各个数据包中并限制包大小；(3) 双层双分支自回归结构，缩短依赖链以限制丢失数据包引发的级联错误。

Result: 在20%丢包率下，该方法相比LossResilientLIC平均PSNR提升1.84 dB，PSNR方差降低一个数量级。仅用均匀随机丢失训练的模型能够泛化到Gilbert-Elliott信道建模的突发性丢包场景，表现优于专门针对该条件训练的现有方法。

Conclusion: 实验证明该方法在重建质量和稳定性上均优于现有方法，且具有良好的泛化能力，能够应对实际通信中复杂多变的丢包模式。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) has achieved impressive rate-distortion performance. However, existing methods remain highly vulnerable to packet loss, a common challenge in satellite and emergency communications. This vulnerability stems from non-uniform information distribution at the packetization stage and sequential decoding dependencies at the entropy coding stage. We propose an end-to-end loss-resilient image compression scheme that addresses both. Before packetization, we introduce an Inter-Channel Redistribution (ICR) mechanism to redistribute channel energy, preventing critical information concentrating in a small subset of channels. Then, an Interleaved Channel Grouping (ICG) strategy partitions latent channels in a strided manner to disperse information across packets, with each packet kept within constrained sizes. To limit cascading errors from lost packets, we adopt a two-layer dual-branch autoregressive structure to shorten the dependency chain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing approaches in both reconstruction quality and stability. At 20% packet loss, it achieves an average PSNR gain of 1.84 dB over LossResilientLIC while reducing PSNR variance by an order of magnitude. Notably, trained under uniform random loss only, our model generalizes to bursty loss modeled by the Gilbert-Elliott channel, outperforming methods explicitly trained for such conditions.

</details>


### [110] [AlbumentationsX: One Augmentation Pipeline for Images and Related Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11123)
*Vladimir Iglovikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlbumentationsX是一个数据增强库，通过将所有变换、概率、注释设置和随机种子集中在单个Compose对象中，确保图像及其各类注释（掩码、框、关键点、立体视图、视频帧、体积）在同一随机变换下保持对齐，避免数据错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据增强中，图像和其注释可能接收不同的随机变化（如裁剪坐标不一致），导致数据静默错位，影响模型训练质量。

Method: 将变换列表、概率、注释设置和随机种子封装在一个Compose对象中，每次调用只选择一次随机值并应用于所有支持的训练样本组件；保持对象掩码、框和标签的一致性，支持自定义变换，并能保存流水线定义、展示单次调用过程及重放。

Result: 在文件解码为数组之后、PyTorch分批之前执行声明的变换，确保所有数据类型使用同一随机变换；实践者仍需自行判断翻转、裁剪、颜色变化等操作是否保持标签正确。

Conclusion: AlbumentationsX通过统一的随机种子和变换执行机制，解决了数据增强过程中的对齐一致性问题，同时保留了实践者对增强操作语义正确性的判断空间。

Abstract: Augmentation can corrupt a training example when an image and its annotations receive different random changes. A crop must use the same coordinates for the image, mask, boxes, keypoints, stereo views, video frames, or volume. Code paths that choose these values separately can silently misalign the data.
  AlbumentationsX keeps the transform list, probabilities, annotation settings, and random seed in one Compose object. Each call chooses random values once and applies them to every supported part of the training example. The library keeps each object's mask, box, and label together and lets projects add their own transforms. It can also save the pipeline definition, show what happened in one call, and run that call again.
  The examples place Compose after files have been decoded into arrays and before PyTorch groups examples into a batch. AlbumentationsX executes the declared transforms. Practitioners still decide whether a flip, crop, color change, or other operation preserves the correct label for their task.

</details>


### [111] [Is There Really a Camouflaged Object? Towards Realistic Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11135)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Chenyang Si,Wende Tan,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有伪装目标检测（COD）方法在封闭世界假设下忽略真实场景中存在纯背景或非伪装物体的情况，导致开放环境中产生严重误检的问题，本文提出大规模基准OPC16K（含16,245张来自14个来源的图像）和存在感知的伪装网络OPCNet，将COD从纯分割任务重构为物体定位与伪装存在推理的联合问题，显著减少负样本上的误检同时保持分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有COD方法均基于封闭世界假设，即假设每张输入图像都包含伪装目标，但这忽略了真实世界中存在纯背景图像或非伪装物体图像的情况。这种假设导致现有模型在开放环境中部署时产生严重的误检（假阳性），缺乏对负样本拒绝能力的研究。

Method: 1) 构建OPC16K大规模基准，包含伪装目标图像、纯背景图像、非伪装目标图像三类，支持分割质量和负样本拒绝的综合评估。2) 提出OPCNet，将COD从纯分割任务重构为物体定位与伪装存在推理的联合问题，包含三个关键模块：层次化存在推理（区分CO、BG、NOCOD三种场景）、相似性感知的伪装关系建模（捕获前景-背景伪装线索）、存在感知的特征细化（用存在预测调控分割特征）。

Result: 在OPC16K基准上的大量实验表明，OPCNet在提出的现实COD评估协议下取得了优越性能，在负样本上显著减少假阳性，同时保持对伪装物体的准确分割。

Conclusion: 本文通过提出大规模现实基准OPC16K和存在感知网络OPCNet，填补了COD领域从封闭世界假设向开放世界场景过渡的空白，为真实应用场景下的伪装目标检测提供了更有效的方案。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to segment objects that are visually concealed in their surroundings and has attracted increasing attention in recent years. However, most existing COD methods are developed under a closed-world assumption, where each input image is assumed to contain a camouflaged object. This assumption ignores realistic scenarios with pure backgrounds or non-camouflaged objects, causing existing models to produce severe false positives when deployed in open-world environments. To address this limitation, we propose OPC16K, a large-scale benchmark for realistic COD. OPC16K contains 16,245 images from 14 sources and is carefully organized into camouflaged-object images, pure background images, and non-camouflaged-object images, enabling comprehensive evaluation of both segmentation quality and negative-sample rejection. Based on this benchmark, we further propose OPCNet, a presence-aware camouflage network that reformulates COD from a pure segmentation task into a joint problem of object localization and camouflage existence reasoning. Specifically, OPCNet introduces hierarchical existence reasoning to distinguish CO, BG, and NOCOD scenarios, similarity-aware camouflage relation modeling to capture foreground-background camouflage cues, and existence-aware feature refinement to regulate segmentation features with existence predictions. Extensive experiments on OPC16K demonstrate that OPCNet achieves superior performance under the proposed realistic COD evaluation protocol, significantly reducing false positives on negative samples while maintaining accurate camouflaged-object segmentation. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/OPCOD.

</details>


### [112] [SAR2Agri: Learning SAR Intensity Representations for Agricultural Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11142)
*Moti Rattan Gupta,Anupam Sobti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督学习管道用于农业监测，通过改进时间代理任务（掩码和课程学习）增强SAR对物候特征的捕捉能力，在SICKLE基准上作物类型制图达到84.9% IoU，优于光学基线和现有SAR基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业监测面临复杂的时间、物候和气候动态挑战，对粮食安全至关重要。现有SAR基础模型（如SAR-JEPA、SARMAE等）主要聚焦目标检测、洪水制图和土地覆盖分类，而现有基于光学影像的物候时间代理任务虽在农业下游任务表现良好，但缺少专门针对SAR影像的自监督预训练管道。

Method: 提出首个仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督学习管道，改进时间代理任务，通过引入掩码和课程学习策略来增强预训练管道对SAR影像中物候特征的捕捉能力。

Result: 在SICKLE基准上，最终模型在作物类型制图任务上达到84.9% IoU，比光学基线高15.3个百分点，比现有SAR基线高2.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提出的自监督预训练管道在农业监测应用中有效，特别是作物类型制图方面显著优于光学和现有SAR基线，证明了该方案对SAR强度编码器预训练的有效性。

Abstract: Agricultural monitoring faces unique challenges, arising from the landscape's complex temporal, phenological, and climate dynamics, yet monitoring them is critical for ensuring food security. Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) satellites offer all-weather day-night imaging capability supporting key monitoring tasks including crop type mapping, yield prediction and phenological event detection. Existing multimodal remote sensing foundation models including TerraMind and CopernicusFM learn SAR representations by grounding them in optical imagery using joint encoding and contrastive learning techniques, while SAR-specific foundation models such as SAR-JEPA, SARMAE, and SAR-W-MixMAE primarily focus on target detection, flood mapping, and land cover classification applications. Recent work has introduced phenology inspired temporal pretext tasks with optical imagery which has shown strong performance on agricultural downstream tasks. In this work, we propose the first self-supervised learning pipeline focused on using only SAR intensity imagery for agricultural applications. We improve the temporal pretext tasks through masking and curriculum learning to enhance the pretraining pipeline's ability to capture phenological features from SAR. On the SICKLE benchmark, our final model achieves 84.9% IoU on crop type mapping, outperforming optical baselines (by 15.3 pt) and existing SAR baselines (by 2.2 pt), demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed pipeline for pretraining SAR intensity encoders for agricultural monitoring.

</details>


### [113] [PRMU: A Corpus-Free Benchmark for Person-Centric Knowledge Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11149)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Ziyuan Chen,Wenda Tan,Chenyang Si,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PRMU基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在无原始语料条件下的真实人物中心知识删除场景中的机器遗忘效果，并引入SGPE轻量级无语料遗忘方法作为基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM机器遗忘方法通常假设可以访问原始遗忘和保留语料，但在实际删除场景中这些数据往往不可用，导致现有方法难以应用于真实场景。

Method: 构建PRMU基准，聚焦自然获取的人物相关知识，通过多样化的文本和视觉探针（包括对抗性评估和细粒度局部性分析）来评估遗忘效果；同时提出SGPE方法，采用知识位移、受保护的参数空间编辑和局部性感知的多模态控制来实现无语料遗忘。

Result: 实验表明现有遗忘方法在目标遗忘与知识保留之间存在不利权衡，激进遗忘设置下局部性显著下降，且容易遭受多模态知识再激活攻击；SGPE在目标遗忘、局部性保留和通用多模态效用之间提供了有竞争力的平衡。

Conclusion: PRMU为真实可扩展的多模态机器遗忘研究提供了基准平台，SGPE展示了无语料遗忘的可行性，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in storing and recalling rich person-related knowledge, raising increasing concerns about reliable knowledge removal. However, existing machine unlearning approaches for MLLMs typically assume access to original forget and retain corpora, which are often unavailable in realistic deletion scenarios. To address this limitation, we introduce PRMU, a benchmark for evaluating corpus-free multimodal unlearning under realistic person-centric deletion requests. PRMU focuses on naturally acquired person-related knowledge and evaluates whether models can remove target knowledge while preserving related knowledge through diverse textual and visual probes, including adversarial evaluation and fine-grained locality analysis. To facilitate research in this setting, we further introduce Similarity-Gated Projection Editing (SGPE), a lightweight corpus-free unlearning baseline with knowledge displacement, protected parameter-space editing, and locality-aware multimodal control. Extensive experiments on representative MLLMs reveal that existing unlearning methods often suffer from unfavorable forgetting-locality trade-offs, with significant locality degradation under aggressive forgetting settings, and remain vulnerable to multimodal knowledge reactivation. Meanwhile, SGPE provides a competitive trade-off between target forgetting, locality preservation, and general multimodal utility. We hope PRMU can facilitate future research toward realistic and scalable multimodal machine unlearning. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/PRMU.

</details>


### [114] [CausalSplat: Towards Comprehensive Hierarchical Reasoning in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11150)
*Jiayu Ding,Meilu Song,Yun Chen,Wei Gao,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalSplat框架，将视觉语言模型与3D场景图结合，用于推理3D高斯分割任务，并构建了Causal-LERF和Causal-ScanNet两个基准数据集，以系统评估常识、空间、功能性和反事实推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D高斯泼溅开放词汇场景理解方法仅限于显式查询，难以处理隐式意图、复杂空间约束和常识推理等实际具身交互所需的能力，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: CausalSplat框架将视觉语言模型与3D场景图相结合，将显式结构感知与隐式逻辑推理分离——前者处理3D空间结构信息，后者负责语义和常识层面的逻辑推断。同时构建Causal-LERF和Causal-ScanNet两个基准测试，系统评估各类推理能力。

Result: 在推理基准上达到最先进水平，同时在标准指代和开放词汇3D分割任务上表现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CausalSplat通过解耦显式结构感知与隐式逻辑推理的思路，有效弥补了现有3DGS方法在推理任务上的不足，为具身智能场景理解提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has advanced open vocabulary scene understanding, existing methods remain confined to explicit queries. They struggle to interpret implicit intents, complex spatial constraints, and commonsense reasoning required for practical embodied interactions. To address this gap, we introduce the task of reasoning 3D Gaussian segmentation and construct two benchmarks, Causal-LERF and Causal-ScanNet. These benchmarks systematically evaluate commonsense, spatial, affordance, and counterfactual reasoning. Evaluations reveal that current state of the art methods perform poorly on these reasoning challenges. Therefore, we propose CausalSplat, a framework that integrates vision-language models with 3D scene graphs to disentangle explicit structural perception from implicit logical inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalSplat achieves state of the art performance on our reasoning benchmarks while showing strong generalizability on standard referring and open vocabulary 3D segmentation tasks. Project Page: https://jiayuding031020.github.io/CausalSplat

</details>


### [115] [VidForensics-M1: Meta-Detection Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Temporal Grounding for AI-Generated Video Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11201)
*Bowei Liu,Zheng Lu,Yuhan Bian,Xinchen Zhang,Xingming Shui,Yuesheng Huang,Xuhuan Li,Zihao Liu,Yifan Yang,Jun Zhou,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将元检测引入AI生成视频检测领域，提出VidForensics-M1框架，通过强化学习联合优化预测标签和支撑证据，利用时间定位提供可验证的证据信号，并设计证据引导的奖励再分配机制，实现稳健且可泛化的AI生成视频检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的视频生成检测器主要依赖监督微调或标签级强化学习，粗粒度的监督限制了模型对未见场景和新兴视频生成器的泛化能力。作者旨在通过引入元检测范式，在强化学习中同时优化标签预测和证据生成，提升检测的可靠性与可解释性。

Method: 提出VidForensics-M1框架：(1) 引入元检测范式，联合优化预测标签和支撑证据；(2) 构建自动化数据流水线，通过边界帧条件视频生成模型替换时间片段来生成配对的真实-伪造视频；(3) 提出证据引导的奖励再分配机制，根据证据质量在标签正确的响应中重新分配奖励，实现证据感知的信用分配，既保留可靠标签监督又鼓励学习细粒度时间伪造定位能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，VidForensics-M1有效利用可验证的时间证据，在AI生成视频检测中取得了稳健且可泛化的性能。

Conclusion: 时间定位相比文本理由能提供更客观且可验证的伪造证据，证据引导的奖励再分配机制能够在保留标签监督的同时增强检测器对细粒度伪造定位能力的习得，从而实现更泛化的AI生成视频检测。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation models have significantly improved the realism of synthetic videos, blurring the boundary between generated and authentic content and raising concerns about misinformation. Existing MLLM-based detectors mainly rely on supervised fine-tuning or label-level reinforcement learning, where coarse supervision limits generalization to unseen scenarios and emerging video generators. To overcome these limitations, we are the first to introduce \textbf{meta-detection} into AI-generated video detection, enabling reliable forgery detection by jointly optimizing predicted labels and supporting evidence within reinforcement learning. This paradigm requires reliable evidence signals and effective mechanisms to integrate them into label-level optimization. Textual rationales provide semantic descriptions of forgery artifacts, but their generation and verification depend on external models, making supervision vulnerable to hallucinations and semantic biases. In contrast, temporal grounding provides more objective and verifiable evidence, as manipulated intervals can be precisely controlled during forgery construction. Based on this insight, we propose an automated data construction pipeline that generates paired real-fake videos by replacing temporal segments with boundary-frame-conditioned video generation models. Furthermore, we introduce \textbf{Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution}, which performs evidence-aware credit assignment by redistributing rewards among label-correct responses according to evidence quality. This preserves reliable label supervision while encouraging detectors to acquire fine-grained and verifiable forgery localization capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textbf{VidForensics-M1} effectively leverages verifiable temporal evidence to achieve robust and generalizable AI-generated video detection.

</details>


### [116] [Capturing Uncertainty in Human Motion for Representation Learning in Soccer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11203)
*Yizhou Xu,Lars Bretzner,Tiesheng Wang,Atsuto Maki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于未来运动预测的自监督表征学习框架，用于理解足球场景中的3D骨骼人体运动。通过引入条件模块对离散化的未来运动建模概率分布，显式学习多模态性。实验表明该方法显著提升运动预测精度，且所学表征可有效迁移至多个足球下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动天然具有不确定性，单一的未来预测无法充分捕捉运动动态的复杂性。作者希望通过对多种可能未来的建模来学习更有效的运动表征，以应对这一本质挑战。

Method: 提出一个用于运动预测的条件模块，在3D欧几里得空间中对离散化未来运动建模概率分布，利用未来轨迹作为显式监督信号来学习多模态性，并以未来运动预测作为自监督学习目标。

Result: 在大规模足球运动员跟踪数据上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了运动预测精度，并且学习到的表征能够有效迁移到多个足球下游应用，展现出强跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模多模态未来运动分布，自监督运动预测框架不仅能提升预测准确性，还能学习到具有良好泛化能力的运动表征，为足球等复杂动态场景中的人体运动理解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a self-supervised representation learning framework for understanding 3D skeleton-based human motion in soccer, using future motion prediction as the learning objective. Since human motion is inherently uncertain, accounting for multiple plausible futures is essential for capturing the underlying motion dynamics and learning effective representations. To this end, we introduce a conditioning module for motion prediction that models a probabilistic distribution over discretized future motions in 3D Euclidean space, learning multimodality with explicit supervision from future trajectories. Experiments on large-scale soccer player tracking data show that our approach substantially improves motion prediction accuracy. Moreover, the learned representations effectively transfer to multiple soccer downstream applications, demonstrating strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [117] [AdvFD: Boosting Visual Generation via Adversarial Fr'echet Distance Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11205)
*Mingju Gao,Jingkai Zhou,Kun Gai,Changqian Yu,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了对抗式Fréchet距离（AdvFD），通过引入可学习的对抗表示空间来弥补静态预训练特征空间的不足，解决了Fréchet后训练中的Fréchet hacking问题，并采用真实特征白化来稳定min-max优化，在多个骨干网络和模型规模上取得了持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fréchet距离作为生成器后训练的分布级目标已显示有效性，但直接优化Fréchet目标会导致Fréchet hacking——目标指标持续改善，而视觉质量和在其他特征空间的Fréchet对齐却停滞或恶化。作者将这一问题归因于现有Fréchet损失使用的静态预训练特征空间，这些空间提供了不完整且固定的真实与生成分布差异视图。

Method: AdvFD在静态FD-Loss基础上引入一个可学习的对抗表示空间：该表示通过对抗方式最大化真实样本与生成样本之间的Fréchet差异，而生成器则在自适应特征空间中最小化相同差异。为了防止对抗表示通过特征放大平凡地增大目标，进一步引入真实特征白化，对表示的尺度和协方差几何进行归一化，从而稳定min-max优化过程。

Result: 大量实验表明，AdvFD在JiT和pMF两种骨干网络上以及不同模型规模下，均能一致地改善一步生成器后训练的性能。

Conclusion: AdvFD通过对抗学习的自适应特征空间有效解决了Fréchet hacking问题，为分布级生成器后训练提供了一个更稳健的优化框架。

Abstract: Fréchet distance has recently emerged as an effective distribution-level objective for generator post-training, complementing the conventional sample-level diffusion and flow-matching losses. However, directly optimizing Fréchet objectives can cause Fréchet hacking. The target metrics keep improving, but visual quality and Fréchet alignment in other feature spaces may stagnate or deteriorate. We attribute this failure to the static pretrained feature spaces used by existing Fréchet losses. These feature spaces provide incomplete and fixed views of the differences between real and generated distributions. To address this limitation, we propose Adversarial Fréchet Distance (AdvFD), which complements the static representation targets in FD-Loss with a calibrated adversarially learned representation. AdvFD augments the original static Fréchet objective with a learnable representation that adversarially maximizes the Fréchet discrepancy between real and generated samples, while the generator minimizes the same discrepancy in the resulting adaptive feature space. To prevent the adversarial representation from trivially increasing the objective through feature amplification, we further introduce real-feature whitening, which normalizes its scale and covariance geometry and stabilizes the min--max optimization. Extensive experiments show that AdvFD consistently improves one-step generator post-training across both JiT and pMF backbones and across different model scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [LLM Agents Factory: Retrieval of Domain-Specific LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09934)
*Vitalii Belov,Artyom Sosedka,Andrey Sakhovskiy,Elizaveta Kovtun,Artyom Boyarskikh,Semen Budennyy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM Agents Factory是一个基于检索的框架，通过预先构建的超过2万个智能体档案库，按需构造领域特定且基于Wikipedia的智能体，在保持与AutoGen（120B骨干）相当准确度的同时显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过角色分工提升任务性能，但动态生成智能体面临计算成本高和稳定性不足的问题，难以满足工业应用的严格需求。

Method: 构建包含超过2万条预定义智能体档案的检索式框架，支持两种模式：（1）通过语义搜索检索智能体档案；（2）将档案蒸馏为紧凑模型，微调后直接生成智能体。

Result: 在MMLU、BIG-bench和BIG-bench Hard的单一智能体场景实验中，检索式智能体构建在准确率上超越非智能体基线，同时以显著更低的推理成本达到与120B骨干AutoGen相当的质量。

Conclusion: 从结构化智能体仓库中进行检索，是动态智能体生成的一种成本高效、准确且可控的替代方案，能够满足工业应用对效率和稳定性的严格要求。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents improve task performance by decomposing problems into role-specialized behaviors. However, their practical deployment is often limited by the computational cost and instability associated with the on-the-fly agent design for each user request. To address this, we present LLM Agents Factory, a retrieval-based framework that constructs domain-specific and Wikipedia-grounded agents on demand using a base of over 20K predetermined agent profiles. Our framework supports two modes: (1) agent profile retrieval via semantic search and (2) distillation into a compact model fine-tuned for direct agent generation. Experiments on MMLU, BIG-bench, and BIG-bench Hard in a single-agent scenario demonstrate that our retrieval-based agent construction surpasses non-agent baselines in accuracy while matching AutoGen generation quality with a 120B backbone at a substantially lower inference cost. Our work reveals that retrieval from a structured agent repository provides a cost-efficient, accurate, and controllable alternative to dynamic agent generation, responding to the strict demands of industrial applications. We provide the implementation code and the agent base in https://huggingface.co/frontier-ai/llm-agent-factory.

</details>


### [119] [Conflict or Strategy? Asymmetric Role Framing of La France insoumise and Rassemblement National in French News Headlines, 2022-2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09936)
*Amr Sobhy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了2022-2025年间法国25家媒体发布的28,592条关于"不屈法国"（LFI）和"国民联盟"（RN）的新闻标题，通过三模型LLM注释流水线和分层人工审计，发现左翼与右翼民粹主义挑战者在新闻框架中呈现"角色不对称"而非"价值不对称"：LFI更多出现在冲突框架中，RN更多出现在战略选举框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究法国新闻标题是否将左右翼民粹主义挑战者视为对称的"极端分子"，还是作为本质上不同的政治对手，以理解媒体框架中潜在的政治偏见。

Method: 收集2022-2025年25家法语媒体关于LFI和RN的28,592条标题，采用三模型LLM注释流水线进行标注（冲突框架、战略博弈框架、去合法化、角色分配、道德归责等维度），并通过分层人工审计验证，辅以自助法和置换检验确保结果稳健性。

Result: 发现角色不对称而非价值不对称：冲突框架和战略博弈框架在模型和时间维度上比去合法化更稳健，AGGRESSOR作为佐证角色语法。LFI更多出现在冲突框架中，RN更多出现在战略选举框架中，该差异在所有注释模型中方向一致，且跨媒体类型和大部分时间段保持稳健。道德归责层（谁被指责、合法化或视为受害者）由媒体而非政党决定，聚合层面呈现无效结果但掩盖了语料库中高度两极化的模式。

Conclusion: 论文提出政治角色分配作为计算框架研究的分析目标，解构了基于价值衡量的混淆，并建立了分层可靠性框架以校准政治文本任务中的多数投票LLM注释流水线。

Abstract: Do French news headlines frame left- and right-populist challengers as symmetric ``extremes,'' or as fundamentally different political adversaries? We examine 28,592 headlines about La France insoumise (LFI) and Rassemblement National (RN) published by 25 French-language outlets between 2022 and 2025, annotated through a three-model LLM pipeline validated against a stratified human audit. The clearest finding is role asymmetry rather than valence asymmetry: conflict framing and strategic-game framing are more robust across models and time than delegitimization, with AGGRESSOR serving as corroborating role syntax. LFI appears in headlines more often through a conflict register and RN through a strategic-electoral register. This role gap is direction-stable across all three annotation models, survives bootstrapping and permutation tests, and persists across outlet families and most of 2022-2025. A secondary moral-accounting layer (who is blamed, legitimized, or cast as a victim) is structured by outlet rather than party, producing aggregate nulls that conceal some of the corpus's most polarized patterns. Methodologically, the annotation pipeline reveals a two-tier reliability profile: conflict and strategic-game framing achieve the strongest human validation and cross-model stability; actor role is direction-stable but treated as corroborating because its audit reliability is lower; normative-judgment constructs (legitimacy, blame) are weaker. The paper contributes political-role assignment as a target for computational framing research that decomposes what valence-based measures conflate, and establishes a construct-stratified reliability framework for calibrating majority-vote LLM annotation pipelines in political text tasks.

</details>


### [120] [Carefully Considering Culture: Analyzing LLM Alignment in Single- and Multi-Cultural Settings using Cultural Consensus Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09937)
*Krishna Pothugunta,John P. Lalor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用文化共识理论（CCT）对大型语言模型在不同国家文化规范理解中的表现进行建模分析，发现模型常因缺乏共识凝聚或过度正则化共识而曲解文化结构，并提出通过组内方差显式表征来诊断模型是否反映真实人类多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP研究在探测大语言模型对不同国家文化规范的理解时，仅关注分布性模式，忽视了群体共识或国家内的多元文化环境（如多文化共存），需要一种能捕捉文化多维细微差别的建模方法。

Method: 借鉴文化人类学中的文化共识理论（CCT），对10个国家和12个领域的世界价值观调查（WVS）数据应用CCT模型，通过显式表征组内方差来评估语言模型对文化结构的反映情况。

Result: 研究发现模型经常错误表征文化结构，主要表现为两种失败模式：无法形成凝聚的共识，或过度正则化共识（即过度简化文化多样性），导致模型无法准确反映真实的文化多元面貌。

Conclusion: CCT提供了可操作的诊断工具，能够区分模型反映真实人类多样性还是算法同质化，为评估和改善大语言模型的文化理解能力提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: Recent work in NLP has probed large language models for their understanding of cultural norms across countries. However, this work typically considers distributional patterns, ignoring group consensus or possible multicultural environments within a country. In this work, we leverage cultural consensus theory (CCT) from cultural anthropology to model such multidimensional nuance. Applying CCT to the World Values Survey (WVS) across 10 countries and 12 domains, we demonstrate that models frequently misrepresent cultural structures by either failing to form cohesive consensus or severely over-regularizing consensus. Through explicit representation of intra-group variance, CCT provides actionable diagnostics to evaluate when models reflect true human diversity versus algorithmic homogenization.

</details>


### [121] [The Multilingual Quantization Tax: Structural Collapse and Typological Fragility in Edge SLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09941)
*Mohammad Wathiq Soualhi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Gemma 4和Qwen 3.5架构进行了零样本多语言4-bit量化评估，覆盖8种类型学多样的语言，揭示了量化退化在不同语言和架构间的非均匀分布，并识别出四种关键现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4-bit权重量化对于在边缘设备上部署小型语言模型至关重要，但现有性能退化评估 overwhelmingly 以英语为中心，缺乏多语言视角下的系统性分析。

Method: 在Gemma 4和Qwen 3.5两个架构上，使用MMLU ProX Lite和GlobalPIQA基准，对8种类型学上多样的语言进行零样本4-bit量化评估。

Result: 识别出四种现象：(1)类型学脆弱性：低资源和非拉丁文字出现表征崩溃，且具有架构特异性双分离；(2)母语脆弱悖论：预训练通路对精度损失保护有限；(3)领域特异性遗忘：跨语言路由的多步任务退化，而关联性soft-science回忆保持稳健；(4)量化抗性：高度饱和且类型学对齐的领域抵抗确定性退化，量化后性能提升受统计噪声限制。

Conclusion: 参数截断暴露了深层预训练不平等性，量化退化并非均匀分布，而是与语言资源水平、类型学特征和领域类型密切相关。

Abstract: While 4-bit weight quantization is critical for deploying Small Language Models (SLMs) on edge devices, evaluations of the resulting performance degradation-the quantization tax-remain overwhelmingly English-centric. We present a zero-shot multilingual evaluation of 4-bit quantization across the Gemma 4 and Qwen 3.5 architectures. Evaluating on eight typo-logically diverse languages using MMLU ProX Lite and GlobalPIQA, we show parameter truncation exposes deep pre-training inequalities. We identify four phenomena: (1) Typological Fragility: low-resource and specific non-Latin scripts suffer representational collapse via architecture-specific double dissociations, failing to generate valid task logits; (2) Home Language Fragility Paradox: foundational pre-training pathways provide limited precision loss protection; (3) Domain-Specific Forgetting: multi-step cross-lingual routing degrades while associative soft-science recall remains robust; and (4) Quantization Resistance: highly saturated, typologically aligned domains resist deterministic degradation, with post-quantization performance gains bounded by statistical noise.

</details>


### [122] [When Chain-of-Thought Helps and When It Hurts: An Empirical Investigation of the Serial-Depth Bottleneck in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09942)
*Tughanbulut Kurtulush*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过H_dp带宽约束理论框架，系统研究链式思维（CoT）提示是否普遍提升LLM推理能力。研究发现CoT并非通用推理增强器，而是带宽旁路：它仅对超出Transformer单次前向传播能力的高串行深度任务有效（如GSM8K、MATH，恢复差距+54至+68个百分点），对浅层任务（如MMLU、ARC）结构性冗余（Delta在0至+4.6个百分点），且对中间复杂度任务（HumanEval）呈现模型规模依赖的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"CoT提示普遍提升LLM推理能力"这一广泛假设。通过H_dp带宽界理论框架识别真实的架构瓶颈——超出Transformer单次前向传播能力的串行计算必须外部化，这正是CoT的作用机制。作者希望证明CoT的效果具有任务深度依赖性，而非普适增强。

Method: 基于H_dp带宽界（Chen et al., 2024）理论框架，提出"基准内串行深度梯度"假设：单次前向传播（无CoT）准确率随每项串行深度单调下降，而CoT近似深度不变。在三个指令微调模型（Qwen-2.5-7B/32B、Llama-3.1-8B）和五个标准NLP基准上，于实际上下文长度下测量CoT效应，并进行McNemar显著性检验与Spearman相关性分析。研究在OSF上预注册。

Result: 跨基准深度-恢复相关性Spearman rho=0.661（p=0.007，n=15）；15个基准级McNemar检验中9个在Bonferroni校正后显著。高深度P完全任务（GSM8K、MATH）上CoT恢复差距+54至+68个百分点（所有模型）；浅层TC^0任务（MMLU、ARC）上CoT冗余（Delta 0至+4.6个百分点）；中间复杂度L类（HumanEval）呈模型大小依赖转变（32B:+23.2 pp，8B:+9.1 pp，7B:-28.7 pp）。但ARC高无CoT准确率（高达95%）可能反映数据污染，使该零结果并非干净的架构测试。

Conclusion: CoT不是通用推理增强器，而是带宽旁路机制：它帮助超出单次前向传播容量的串行计算，对已能容纳的任务冗余。研究为理解CoT的作用边界提供了理论依据和实证证据，同时指出了数据污染对基准测试效度的影响。

Abstract: It is widely assumed that chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting universally improves LLM reasoning. We investigate this through the conceptual framework of the H_dp bandwidth bound (Chen et al., 2024): although the formal bound binds only asymptotically (at astronomically large prompt lengths), it identifies a real architectural bottleneck -- serial computation exceeding a transformer's single-pass capacity must be externalised, which is what CoT does. Our central finding is a within-benchmark serial-depth gradient: single-pass (no-CoT) accuracy degrades monotonically with per-item serial depth, while CoT is approximately depth-invariant. We measure CoT effects across three instruction-tuned models (Qwen-2.5-7B/32B, Llama-3.1-8B) and five standard NLP benchmarks at practical context lengths. On high-depth P-complete tasks (GSM8K, MATH), CoT gives a +54 to +68 pp recovery gap across all models. On shallow TC^0 tasks (MMLU, ARC), CoT is structurally redundant (Delta in [0.0, +4.6] pp, no significant negative effect) -- though high no-CoT baselines (up to 95% on ARC) may reflect contamination, so this null is not a clean architectural test. The intermediate class L (HumanEval) shows a model-size-dependent transition: +23.2 pp (32B), +9.1 pp (8B), -28.7 pp (7B). The cross-benchmark depth-recovery correlation is Spearman rho = 0.661 (p = 0.007, n = 15); 9 of 15 benchmark-level McNemar tests are significant after Bonferroni correction. Pre-registered on OSF, our results indicate that CoT is not a universal reasoning enhancer but acts as a bandwidth bypass: it helps serial computation that strains single-pass capacity and is redundant for tasks that already fit.

</details>


### [123] [Position Encoding in Transformers: From Absolute and Relative Methods to Rotary Position Embeddings and Long-Context Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10021)
*Jiguo Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇技术综述，系统梳理了Transformer中的各类位置编码方法，从绝对位置嵌入、相对位置表示到RoPE等现代方法，并深入探讨了多种长上下文扩展技术及其评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力机制本身不编码token顺序，位置编码是Transformer架构的关键组成部分。但随着上下文窗口不断扩展，如何设计既能有效编码位置信息又能支持长上下文泛化的方法成为重要课题。

Method: 采用统一框架比较正弦/学习式绝对位置嵌入、Shaw式相对位置表示、Transformer-XL、T5相对位置偏置、ALiBi和RoPE等方法，从注入位置、计算成本、KV缓存兼容性和长度外推等维度进行分析；并详细梳理Position Interpolation、NTK-aware缩放、YaRN、LongRoPE等一系列长上下文扩展方法。

Result: 揭示了各种位置编码方法的核心差异与权衡，指出RoPE通过将绝对位置索引转换为Query-Key内积中的相对相位差来工作；同时系统总结了各长上下文扩展方法在频率分配、注意力重缩放、训练长度和目标上下文长度方面的特点。

Conclusion: 关键结论是：位置特征的计算能力超过训练长度并不意味着可靠的长上下文泛化，上下文扩展必须通过短上下文保持、逐位置困惑度、检索、推理和长上下文代码任务等多维度评估来验证。

Abstract: Self-attention models content-dependent interactions between tokens but does not by itself encode token order. Position encoding addresses this limitation by introducing absolute coordinates, relative distances, or position-dependent rotations into Transformer representations and attention scores. This technical survey develops a unified account of sinusoidal and learned absolute position embeddings, Shaw-style relative position representations, Transformer-XL, T5 relative position bias, ALiBi, and Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE). We derive how RoPE converts absolute position indices into relative phase differences in Query-Key inner products and compare these methods in terms of where position is injected, computational cost, compatibility with KV caching, and length extrapolation. We then examine long-context extensions, including Position Interpolation, RoPE scaling laws, NTK-aware scaling, Dynamic NTK, NTK-by-parts, YaRN, LongRoPE, and LongRoPE2, with emphasis on frequency allocation, attention rescaling, training length, and target context length. We also summarize implementation considerations, evaluation protocols, and position-encoding choices in representative large language models. A central conclusion is that the ability to compute positional features beyond the training length does not imply reliable long-context generalization; context extension must be evaluated through short-context retention, position-wise perplexity, retrieval, reasoning, and long-context code tasks.

</details>


### [124] [PERCEPT: A Corpus for POS Tagging and Analysis of Persian-English Code-Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10109)
*Ghazal Kalhor,Zahra Jafari,Amirarsalan Shahbazi,Behnam Bahrak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PERCEPT，这是第一个公开可用的大规模波斯语-英语代码混合语料库，包含6,800条来自X、Instagram和Digikala的帖子，并带有Universal Dependencies POS标注。论文还提出了一个LLM辅助标注框架，并通过人工评估验证了标注可靠性，最后对跨平台的代码混合模式进行了全面的语言学分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有多个语言对的代码混合语料库，但波斯语-英语代码混合领域研究不足。现有波斯语资源缺乏对代码混合词汇的Universal Dependencies (UD) POS标注，这限制了语言学分析和语法感知NLP模型的开发。

Method: 构建了包含6,800条帖子的PERCEPT语料库（来自X、Instagram和Digikala三个平台），提出了一种LLM辅助的自动标注框架，该框架能够自动分配POS标签和文档级主题标签，并通过人工评估验证标注质量。

Result: 人工评估显示自动标注与黄金标注之间的一致性很高，证明了标注的可靠性。语言学分析发现：名词是代码混合词汇的主导类别，其他POS类别的分布因平台而异；代码混合词汇的位置分布跨平台高度一致，而触发效应在Digikala中显著更明显。

Conclusion: PERCEPT填补了波斯语-英语代码混合语料库的空白，为语言学分析和语法感知NLP模型开发提供了宝贵的资源。该语料库公开可用，为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Social media has become a major venue for multilingual communication, where users frequently mix multiple languages within a single utterance. Although code-mixed corpora have been developed for several language pairs, Persian-English code-mixing remains relatively underexplored. Existing Persian resources lack Universal Dependencies (UD) part-of-speech (POS) annotations for code-mixed words, limiting both linguistic analyses and the development of syntax-aware NLP models. To address this gap, we introduce PERCEPT, the first publicly available large-scale Persian-English code-mixed corpus annotated with Universal Dependencies POS tags for code-mixed words. The dataset comprises 6,800 posts collected from X, Instagram, and Digikala. We further present an LLM-assisted annotation framework that automatically assigns POS tags and document-level topics. Human evaluation demonstrates high agreement between the automatically generated annotations and gold annotations, confirming the reliability of the annotations. Using PERCEPT, we conduct the first comprehensive linguistic analysis of Persian-English code-mixing across multiple social media platforms. Our analyses reveal that nouns are the predominant category for code-mixed words, while the distributions of other POS categories vary across platforms. We further find that the positional distribution of code-mixed words is remarkably consistent across platforms, whereas the triggering effect is substantially more pronounced in Digikala. PERCEPT is publicly available at https://github.com/kalhorghazal/PERCEPT.

</details>


### [125] [The Parser Already Knows: Lightweight Bias Correction in Constrained Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10137)
*Işıl Özgü,Yaoxuan Wu,Guy Van den Broeck,Miryung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级、离线训练的logit修正方法，利用增量解析过程中已维护的内部解析器/词法分析器状态来恢复被语法约束解码（GCD）掩盖掉的语言模型真实概率分布，在几乎不增加推理开销的情况下同时提升输出质量与推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCD方法通过硬性掩码强制语言模型输出语法合规内容，但会扭曲模型的概率分布，导致生成结果偏差；而在线采样虽能恢复分布却计算开销巨大。现有方法不得不在输出质量和推理延迟之间做出取舍，亟需一种既能恢复分布又开销极低的方案。

Method: 利用增量解析过程中天然维护的解析器与词法分析器状态（这些状态本身已被用于掩码计算）作为条件特征，结合候选下一个token，训练一个轻量级的离线logit修正器。该修正器不修改基础语言模型的任何权重，仅对mask后的logit进行校正以恢复原始分布。最简变体仅依赖候选token本身，利用其隐含的前瞻信息进行修正。

Result: 在多种语法上，该修正方法显著缩小了掩码分布与语言模型真实分布之间的差距，一致地优于纯掩码和在线采样方法。即使是最轻量级的变体（仅依赖候选token），也能达到或超过两种基线方法的表现。

Conclusion: 通过利用增量解析中已有的句法与词法状态来恢复被掩码移除的概率质量，该方法成功调和了语言模型的概率完整性与语法合规性之间的矛盾，在不牺牲推理效率的前提下提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Grammar Constrained Decoding (GCD) forces Language Models (LMs) to produce syntactically valid outputs by masking out non-conforming tokens at each step. However, rigid masking distorts the model's underlying probability distribution, often biasing generation toward valid but suboptimal outputs. While online sampling restores this distribution, it requires computationally expensive iterative resampling. As a result, existing methods force a compromise between output quality and inference latency. Our key insight is that the internal parser and lexer states inherently maintained during incremental parsing already encode future grammatical validity -- exactly the information required to restore the LM's true distribution. We propose a lightweight, offline-trained logit correction conditioned on this syntactic and lexical state together with candidate next tokens. Because these states are already computed as a necessary part of incremental parsing for masking, extracting them adds negligible overhead while leaving the base LM's weights completely untouched. Across several grammars, this correction substantially closes the gap between the masked distribution and the LM's true distribution, consistently outperforming both masking and online sampling. Even its lightest variant, which relies on the candidate next token alone, still matches or exceeds both baselines: the next token itself carries an implicit lookahead, much like how parsers commonly use a lookahead token to resolve ambiguous decisions. By restoring the probability mass that masking removes, it reconciles the LM's probabilistic integrity with grammar conformance.

</details>


### [126] [Multimodal Item Parameter Estimation using Simulated Response Probabilitie](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10154)
*Christopher Ormerod,YoungKoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用基于Qwen3.5微调的多模态大语言模型，通过让学生在不同能力水平下对包含图像和文本的多项选择题作答，来重建多选模型（MCM）和三参数逻辑斯蒂（3PL）模型曲线，并直接从模型预测的选项概率中准确估计题目难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统项目反应理论（IRT）模型如3PL和MCM在估计题目难度时通常需要大量学生作答数据，且难以有效利用多模态（图像+文本）题目的信息。研究者希望通过大语言模型隐式学习学生作答模式，从而更高效、更准确地重建这些IRT曲线并估计题目难度。

Method: 基于Qwen3.5微调一个多模态大语言模型，在包含图像和文本刺激的多项选择题大规模训练语料上进行训练，并以标记的学生能力水平为条件。模型被提示和微调以复现学生在不同能力水平下的选项选择概率，通过学习离散能力范围内学生的系统性错误模式，隐式捕获3PL和MCM曲线所编码的响应概率。

Result: 在保留测试集上，模型能够准确近似题目难度，证明了微调后的多模态LLM能够有效重建3PL和MCM曲线并从中提取题目难度估计。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型能够通过隐式学习学生的响应概率模式，在无需传统IRT参数估计流程的情况下，直接基于预测的选项概率准确估计题目难度，为智能教育和评测领域提供了一种新颖且有效的方法。

Abstract: We present results from reconstructing multiple-choice model (MCM) and three-parameter logistic (3PL) model curves using a fine-tuned multimodal large language model (LLM) based on Qwen3.5. The model is prompted and fine-tuned to replicate choice probabilities across a large training corpus of multiple-choice items containing both image and text stimuli, conditioned on a labeled set of student ability levels. By learning to reproduce the systematic error patterns of students across a discrete range of abilities, the LLM implicitly captures the underlying response probabilities encoded in the 3PL and MCM curves. This allows us to accurately approximate item difficulty on a held-out test set directly from the model's predicted option probabilities.

</details>


### [127] [Similarity Gates Approve Reversals: A Validity Audit of Embedding-Cosine Thresholds in Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10216)
*Scott E. Frias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审计了Agent框架中基于嵌入余弦相似度的文本质量门（去重、语义缓存、漂移防护、答案评分等），发现其测量的是"措辞变化程度"而非"语义是否相同"。在反义/反向指令等关键场景中，评分方向与语义真实方向相反，导致安全检查反向失效。实测中漂移防护门对56个语义破坏性变异0捕获，甚至"停用"与"改用"研究药物这种相反指令达到0.9608的高余弦分。作者提出配对审计（matched-pair audit）方法，发现最优配置AUROC 0.79-0.90，并发布语料与方法供复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent框架中大量部署基于嵌入余弦相似度的质量门，但该测量工具回答的实际上是"措辞改变了多少"而非"语义是否改变"。作者通过审计发现这两个问题在门控设计用于捕获的场景中可能完全反向运行，导致安全检查反向失效且评估结果被混淆因子污染。

Method: 作者对90个配置-阈值-任务单元进行平衡准确率评估，审计5个已部署操作点；设计配对审计（matched-pair audit）以控制文本重叠度混淆因子；构建2x2平衡语料设计（反向vs同义、措辞变化）；测试编码器替换、重叠条件门控和NLI替代方案等多种修复策略。

Result: 平衡准确率中位数0.525，从未超过0.700；漂移防护门捕获0/56语义破坏变异；反向指令获0.9608高余弦分；朴素语料下18个配置-任务单元中有13个AUROC恰为0.000；重叠条件门控在样本内0.750但留出数据仅0.533；最强配置在匹配重叠下AUROC达0.79-0.90，但仅配对审计能揭示部署机制。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入余弦相似度并以固定阈值截断的质量门测量的是错误对象，在安全关键场景中可能反向运行。直接修复方案（编码器替换、重叠门控、NLI）均失败，但嵌入仍具潜力，可通过配对审计构建有效测量工具。

Abstract: Agent frameworks ship quality gates that compare text blocks by embedding-cosine similarity and decide at a fixed cutoff. Deduplication filters, semantic caches, drift guards, and answer grader gates deploy to answer the question: "Does this text still mean the same thing?" But the score answers a different question: "How much did the wording change?" We audit this gate class as a measurement instrument. In the cases these gates exist to catch, the two can run in opposite ways. Many times, reversing an instruction is a single word edit, while agreement often rephrases a sentence. The consequence is a safety check that fires backwards. The production drift guard we audited caught 0 of 56 meaning-breaking mutations, and one approved item, "withhold the study drug" -> "administer the study drug", came in at cosine 0.9608. We observed five shipped operating points, and balanced accuracy across 90 configuration-threshold-task cells never exceeded 0.700 (median 0.525). The same confounder also corrupted evaluations. A naively built corpus inherits this confounder and can return an inverted verdict, with a decision AUROC exactly 0.000 in 13 of 18 configuration-task cells (at most 0.040 in all 18) against 0.440-0.815 for the same nine configurations under a balanced 2x2 design. Twice in the effort it captured our own headline claims. Obvious repairs fail: an encoder swap and an overlap-conditioned gate (0.750 in-sample, 0.533 held-out) land at chance on separately authored held-out data, and an NLI drop-in did no better. Embeddings do still bear hope here, as the strongest two of nine configurations separated reversal from paraphrase at matched overlap (AUROC 0.79-0.90), but only a matched-pair audit reveals the deployment regime. We release the corpus method, harness, and frozen results, and contend that scores gated this way measure the wrong thing. We believe a valid instrument is buildable.

</details>


### [128] [Off-Axis, On Purpose: Where a Transformer Computes Concepts and Why it Does So](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10251)
*Mark Oskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer模型的中间状态在训练中自发维持在一个与输出读出方向近似正交的子空间中，这一"离轴"位置并非障碍而是功能性设计。模型分两个阶段计算：概念阶段（离轴操作，隔离词汇与组合）和答案阶段（近轴读出）。强制将层投影到读出方向会压缩概念工作空间但不影响传统基准指标。该几何结构可以被主动施加——通过插入单个固定旋转而非损失函数约束，即可获得基线质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的中间表示大部分偏离其最终输出方向（unembedding读出的轴），这种"离轴"位置通常被视为解释障碍。作者试图证明这种偏移不是噪声，而是具有功能意义的几何结构，并探索其是否可以被主动设计和控制。

Method: 在12层模型上测量各子层输出与读出方向的夹角（75-96度），通过旋转操作比较破坏性程度（64-84倍于随机旋转），区分跨token混合与词汇隔离的作用。通过"early exit"式训练（压层到读出方向）测试几何压缩效果，并通过损失函数约束、相位边界固定旋转、稀疏可吸收旋转等多种方式尝试主动施加该几何结构，跨多个随机种子验证。

Result: ①中间状态离轴是功能性的：破坏离轴子空间导致的损伤完全来自跨token组合而非词表映射。②early-exit训练可将概念工作空间从25个有效维度压缩到14个，但困惑度、LAMBADA、BLiMP等基准完全无法察觉。③通过损失函数施加几何是彩票（6/8种子崩溃）；在相位边界插入固定旋转可行；稀疏可吸收旋转全部9种子收敛（普通训练仅5/9）；随机基座被采纳且质量不变（25次运行，13种旋转）。

Conclusion: Transformer的两阶段几何结构（离轴概念阶段+近轴答案阶段）是可理解、可操控的设计空间。该结构是训练中的一种自由度——不同种子可用不同近正交框架达到相同读出一致性。传统基准无法捕捉这种几何差异，需要新的评估维度。几何约束可以通过架构级插入（如相位边界固定旋转）而非纯损失惩罚来实现。

Abstract: A transformer's answer lives on one axis: the direction its unembedding reads. Its intermediate states largely do not, and that off-axis position is usually treated as an obstacle to interpretation. We show it is functional. A 12-layer model computes in two phases. Through the first, every sublayer writes into a subspace held near-orthogonal to the read-out, attention 75 to 96 degrees off it at every depth. Moving attention's values onto the read-out is 64 to 84 times more damaging than a matched random rotation, and the damage is entirely in cross-token mixing: the subspace insulates composition from the vocabulary.
