Method: ConRub-Med采用三管齐下的策略：(1) 三个异构语言模型独立为每个提示提出原子评估标准，由独立审查模型筛选出三者语义一致的标准；(2) 采用三态评分系统区分正确覆盖、缺失信息和错误主张，错误给予负分而非零分；(3) 当GRPO组内所有响应获得相同最终奖励时，使用成对判断器在双候选顺序一致的前提下提供序列优势，平局组则使用标准GRPO。

Result: 在盲法研究中，两位医学专家评分显示完整管线生成的评估面板比单一生成器更具临床相关性。在9项基准中ConRub-Med排名第一的有6项，取得最高的医学和泛化平均分。基于5,166个提示的评分数据集，在HealthBench-Hard上达到38.98±1.04分，优于InfiMed-ORBIT（8,000样本为33.60，28,000样本为37.30）。

Conclusion: ConRub-Med通过多模型共识构建评分细则、负分三态评分及平局处理机制，在保持临床相关性的同时实现了医学开放问答强化学习的可扩展奖励信号，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has been especially effective in mathematics and coding, where answers can be checked automatically. Many open-ended medical questions lack comparably cheap outcome verifiers: responses may be partly correct, incomplete, or contain clinically consequential errors. Rubrics written or validated by physicians offer strong clinical grounding, but involving experts in every instance is costly. Model-generated rubrics make this supervision scalable. We introduce ConRub-Med to preserve useful distinctions as rubric feedback moves from construction to policy optimization. For each prompt, three heterogeneous language models propose atomic criteria independently; a separate model reviews them, retaining only criteria with semantic support from all three generators. Three-State scoring distinguishes correct coverage, missing information, and incorrect claims. Errors receive negative rather than zero credit. When every response in a complete Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) group receives the same final reward, a pairwise judge provides sequence advantages only if both candidate orders agree, without changing the scalar rewards. Groups without ties use vanilla GRPO. In a blinded study matched by question, two medical experts rate panels from the full pipeline as more clinically relevant than panels produced by one generator. Across the evaluated open models, ConRub-Med ranks first on six of nine benchmarks and achieves the highest medical and generalization averages. Using the resulting rubric dataset of 5,166 prompts, it scores $38.98 \pm 1.04$ (mean $\pm$ SD) on HealthBench-Hard, compared with InfiMed-ORBIT's 33.60 with 8,000 samples and 37.30 with 28,000.

</details>


### [165] [On the Limitations of Cross-Lingual Consistency in Multilingual Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11002)
*Sicheng Zhang,Zhonghao Yan,Binzhu Xie,Shi Qiu,Muzammal Naseer,Naveed Akhtar,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LingT2I基准，覆盖10种常用语言、33K提示词，系统评估文生图模型在跨语言场景下的内容生成与文本渲染表现，揭示了语言不平等、语言依赖的权衡以及语言因素和文化背景对模型输出的系统性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图（T2I）研究大多局限于英文环境，跨语言性能差距和语言特定效应未被充分探索，缺乏系统性基准来评估语言多样化场景下的模型表现。

Method: 构建涵盖10种语言的LingT2I基准（33K提示词），从内容生成和文本渲染两个维度进行跨语言评估；在定量分析基础上，进一步揭示语言依赖的生成模式及其文化背景关联。

Result: 发现了跨语言场景下的语言不平等现象、评估维度间存在语言依赖的权衡取舍，以及多种语言相关的生成模式，表明语言因素和文化语境会系统性影响模型输出。

Conclusion: LingT2I为研究文生图模型的跨语言行为提供了基础，有助于推动开发更稳健、更具包容性的T2I模型。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, existing research has largely focused on English-only settings, leaving cross-lingual performance gaps and language-specific effects insufficiently explored. To fill this gap, we introduce LingT2I, a benchmark covering 10 widely used languages with 33K prompts, designed to evaluate cross-lingual effects in both content generation and text rendering. Building on this benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive cross-lingual analysis, uncovering linguistic inequality and language-dependent trade-offs across evaluation dimensions. Beyond quantitative evaluation, we further reveal a range of language-dependent generation patterns, highlighting how linguistic factors and their corresponding cultural contexts systematically impact model outputs. Our benchmark and analysis provide a foundation for studying cross-lingual behavior in T2I generation and facilitate the development of more robust and inclusive models. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/RISys-Lab/LingT2I.

</details>


### [166] [Templated or fully Synthetic? Prompt construction as a confound in measuring LLM political stance beyond writing assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11008)
*Ilias Chalkidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用完全由LLM生成的合成提示词来替代模板化提示词，用于LLM政治立场检测。研究发现LLM生成的提示词在生态效度上接近真实提示词、明显优于模板化提示词，且避免了模板化提示词因填充文本导致的立场估计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM政治立场检测主要依赖为人类设计的封闭式多项选择题，缺乏真实人机交互的写实性和细微差别，且易受sandbagging影响。IssueBench虽用基于真实聊天日志的模板化提示词做了改进，但模板化提示词仍缺乏真实提示词的细微差别，尤其在开放式任务中，且容易被识别为评估产物。

Method: 将IssueBench扩展至信息寻求和意见分享两个任务；在详细的指令下，以真实提示词为种子，使用LLM生成合成提示词；通过小规模研究（涵盖3个高争议政策议题和3个近期地缘政治冲突），由人类和LLM标注者对真实、模板化、LLM生成的提示词进行生态效度评估；并通过案例研究比较模板化与LLM生成的提示词对同一模型的立场估计差异。

Result: 人类和LLM标注者均认为LLM生成的提示词与真实提示词一样真实，且明显比模板化提示词更真实；LLM生成的提示词能更清晰地传达预期意图和立场；LLMs将模板化提示词与其他两类提示词的区分程度远大于人类标注者。案例研究中，模板化提示词在中性框架下会高估模型沿填充文本编码方向的立场倾向。

Conclusion: 完全合成的LLM生成提示词是模板化提示词的有效替代方案，具有更高的生态效度，能提供更准确的立场估计，尤其避免了模板化提示词在特定框架下引入的立场偏差。

Abstract: Political stance detection in LLMs has long been dominated by closed-ended, multiple-choice political survey questions---originally designed for humans, and thus lacks the realism and nuance of human-AI interactions in the wild, while also being susceptible to sandbagging. The recent IssueBench framework substantially mitigates these limitations with templated prompts anchored in real-world chat logs. Given the rise in non-work-related use of GenAI assistants, we extend IssueBench beyond writing assistance to include two additional tasks, information seeking and opinion sharing. We argue that templated prompts still lack the nuance of real ones, especially for open-ended tasks, and remain recognisable as evaluation artefacts. We propose the use of fully synthetic (LLM-generated) prompts, produced under detailed instructions with real prompts as seeds. We assess the ecological validity of real, templated, and LLM-generated prompts in a small-scale study covering 3 highly contested policy issues and 3 recent geopolitical conflicts. Human and LLM annotators rank LLM-generated prompts as no less realistic than real ones and clearly more realistic than templated ones, and find that they carry their intended intent and stance more clearly; the LLMs separate templated prompts from the other two far more sharply than the humans do. In a case study, templated and LLM-generated prompts yield systematically different stance estimates for the same model, most visibly under neutral framings, where templated prompts overstate the model's leaning in the direction encoded by the topic-and-stance text (filler) slotted into their templates.

</details>


### [167] [Data Attribution of Emergent Misalignment with Persona Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11025)
*Clemens Vetter,David Kaczér,Lucie Flek,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型"涌现性错位"（EM）现象的机制来源，发现通过稀疏自编码器识别出的persona特征（如越狱人格、讽刺、欺骗等）可由微调放大并控制EM，尽管预训练文档中的语义相关内容不足以单独诱导EM，但模型生成的指令-回复对结构能可靠地诱导EM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索EM现象的起源：预训练文档中哪些内容激活相关特征，以及自然人类文本是否足以在窄任务微调后诱导出跨领域的危害行为。这一问题的答案对于理解和防范模型对齐失败至关重要。

Method: 使用基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的模型差异分析方法，在四个开源权重模型上识别被微调放大的persona特征；通过操控单一特征验证其对EM的因果控制作用；将因果特征归因到一百万预训练Web文档语料中；比较人类编写文档与合成指令-回复对在诱导EM方面的效果。

Result: 微调放大了越狱人格、讽刺、欺骗与操控相关特征，抑制安全与助手身份特征；操控单一特征可双向控制EM（诱导率最高62%，超过微调本身的35%）；语义相关的人类文档不足以可靠诱导EM，但由相同内容衍生的合成指令-回复对可诱导EM且能跨模型家族迁移。

Conclusion: 语义相关性本身不足以解释或诱导EM，回复结构或模型生成的措辞在EM诱导中发挥关键作用。这修正了仅基于预训练文档内容特征解释EM的机制观点。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is the phenomenon where fine-tuning a language model on a narrow task leads to harmful behavior in unrelated domains. A leading mechanistic account attributes EM to persona features: latent directions acquired during pre-training that misaligned fine-tuning amplifies. We ask where these features come from: which pre-training documents activate them, and whether naturally occurring human-written text suffices to induce EM. Using Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) based model diffing across four open-weight models, we find that features related to jailbreak personas, sarcasm, deception, and manipulation are amplified by misalignment fine-tuning, while safety-relevant and assistant-identity features are suppressed. Steering individual features controls EM in both directions: it induces misalignment rates of up to 62% in aligned models -- exceeding the 35% reached by misalignment fine-tuning itself -- and re-aligns misaligned models to near-baseline misalignment rates. Attributing the causal features to a corpus of one million pre-training web documents retrieves semantically relevant narratives about villainous characters, domination, and harmful agency. However, fine-tuning on these human-written documents does not reliably induce EM, even after reformatting into assistant-style responses, whereas synthetic instruction-response pairs derived from the same content do -- and transfer across model families. Semantic relevance alone is therefore not sufficient: response structure or model-generated phrasing plays an important role in inducing EM.

</details>


### [168] [myMediWhisper: Construction of Burmese Medical Speech Corpus and Whisper Fine-Tuning for Clinical Dialogue ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11036)
*Ye Kyaw Thu,Ye Bhone Lin,Thura Aung,Htet Arkar,Myat Oo Swe,Thet Htet San,Min Thiha Tun,Thazin Myint Oo,Thepchai Supnithi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于28小时缅甸语医学语音语料库的语音识别框架，通过全参数微调和LoRA参数高效微调Whisper模型，并探索了数据增强对鲁棒性的影响。最优系统（未增强的完全微调myMediWhisper-Medium）在干净语音上取得23.44%的词错误率（WER），达到当前最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper模型虽经大规模多语言预训练，但在缅甸语医学语音上的表现仍然有限。作者旨在构建专门针对缅甸语医学场景的高质量语音识别系统，以弥补该领域研究的空白。

Method: 构建了由母语者录制并验证的28小时高质量缅甸语医学语音语料库；对Whisper模型采用全参数微调（FFT）和基于LoRA的参数高效微调（PEFT）两种方式；在受控噪声和模拟室内声学条件下应用波形级和频谱级数据增强以评估鲁棒性。

Result: 数据增强虽降低了干净语音的性能，但在噪声和混响环境下显著提升了FFT和PEFT设置的鲁棒性。最佳系统为未经增强的全微调myMediWhisper-Medium，在干净语音上取得23.44%的WER，超越了更大的通用领域微调模型。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了针对低资源语言医学领域，利用中等规模Whisper模型进行专门微调可以取得优于大模型的性能，为缅甸语医学语音识别提供了有效的解决方案和公开资源。

Abstract: Although Whisper models benefit from large-scale multilingual pre-training, their performance on Burmese medical speech remains limited. This work presents a Burmese medical speech recognition framework built on a high-quality 28-hour corpus recorded and validated by native speakers. We fine-tune Whisper models using full fine-tuning (FFT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) with LoRA. To evaluate robustness, we apply waveform- and spectrogram-level data augmentation under controlled noise and simulated room acoustics. While augmentation reduces performance on clean speech, it significantly improves robustness in noisy and reverberant environments across FFT and PEFT settings. Our best-performing system, fully fine-tuned myMediWhisper-Medium without augmentation, achieves a state-of-the-art Word Error Rate (WER) of 23.44%, outperforming much larger general-domain fine-tuned models. Dataset and other resources can be found at the Huggingface repository: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LULab/mediTalk-mm-rdy.

</details>


### [169] [TEAMMix: Taxonomy Enrichment Augmentation and Minority-augmented Mixing Strategy for LLM-enhanced Weak-Supervised Hierarchical Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11044)
*Jian Zhang,Zhuohao Yang,Songlin Lei,Bangli Liu,Ziwei Wang,Xufeng Weng,Gehan Amaratunga,Yu Lin,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM数据增强的弱监督层次文本分类（HTC）框架，通过关键词生成与语料挖掘丰富标签层次语义，利用LLM生成伪样本缓解长尾问题，并结合高斯混合模型进行置信度重采样以优化数据质量，在细粒度和不平衡数据集上显著提升了分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次文本分类面临标签层次复杂和类别不平衡等挑战，现有基于大语言模型的方法因提示词冗长、标签结构信息丢失等问题难以高效应用。作者旨在解决LLM在HTC任务中效率低下及结构信息利用不足的局限。

Method: 提出一个弱监督HTC框架：1) 通过关键词生成与语料挖掘在语义上丰富标签层次结构；2) 引导LLM生成伪样本来缓解长尾问题；3) 采用高斯混合模型进行置信度驱动的重采样，优化生成数据的质量。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法有效提升了LLM生成伪标签的可靠性，并在细粒度和不平衡数据集上显著增强了分类性能。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了LLM数据增强在弱监督HTC中的有效性，通过语义丰富标签层次和置信度重采样机制，成功克服了传统LLM方法在结构信息利用和长尾处理上的不足。

Abstract: Hierarchical Text Classification (HTC), as a critical text mining task, faces challenges such as complex label hierarchies and class imbalance. Existing methods based on large language models (LLMs) struggle to be efficiently applied to this task due to issues like lengthy prompts and loss of label structural information. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a weakly supervised HTC framework enhanced by LLM-based data augmentation. The framework first enriches the label hierarchy semantically through keyword generation and corpus mining, thereby enhancing the model's understanding of labels. Subsequently, it guides the LLM to generate pseudo-samples to mitigate the long-tail problem, and employs a Gaussian mixture model for confidence-based resampling to optimize the quality of generated data. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively improves the reliability of LLM-generated pseudo-labels and significantly enhances classification performance on fine-grained and imbalanced datasets.

</details>


### [170] [Multiclass Sentiment Analysis for Identifying Political Viewpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11049)
*Girma Yohannis Bade,Olga Kolesnikova,Jose Luis Oropeza,Grigori Sidorov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究社交媒体上政治观点的多类别情感分析任务，设计了基于XGBoost和BERT两种机器学习方法，在带标签的政治社交媒体帖子数据集上进行训练和评估。实验结果显示XGBoost模型F1分数为0.2835，BERT模型为0.2806，表明政治话语情感分类具有挑战性，为未来多类别政治情感分析研究提供了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体中政治话语快速增长，为分析公众观点和识别不同政治视角提供了机会。情感分析作为NLP核心任务对理解政治话语日益重要，但多类别政治观点情感分析仍具挑战性，需要专门的模型设计与评估研究。

Method: 设计了两种机器学习方法来解决多类别政治观点情感分析任务：一种基于XGBoost，另一种基于BERT。在带标签的政治社交媒体帖子数据集上使用标准分类指标进行训练和评估。

Result: 在测试集上，XGBoost模型达到0.2835的F1分数，BERT模型达到0.2806的F1分数。两个模型的表现都较为有限，反映出该任务的难度。

Conclusion: 实验结果展示了政治话语情感分类（尤其是复杂且语境化的政治文本）的挑战性，同时为未来多类别政治情感分析研究提供了基线参照。

Abstract: The rapid growth of social media has created vast amounts of political discourse, which provides valuable opportunities to analyze public opinions and identify different political perspectives. Sentiment Analysis (SA) is a core task in Natural Language Processing (NLP) that allows the computational study of attitudes and opinions in textual data, and has become increasingly important for understanding political discourse. In this work, we investigate multiclass sentiment analysis of political view- points on social media, that is to automatically discriminate multiple sentiment classes over political issues and figures. To solve this task we design and evaluate two machine-learning approaches based on XGBoost and BERT. We train and evaluate the models on a labeled dataset of political social media posts using standard classification metrics. The experimental results show that the XGBoost model reaches an F1-score of 0.2835 and the BERT- based model reaches an F1-score of 0.2806 on the test set. These results demonstrate the challenge of classifying complex and contextualized political discourse sentiment and provide a baseline for future research in multiclass political sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [171] [Actions Speak Louder than Words: Measuring Cross-Lingual Policy Retention in Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11110)
*Sourabrata Mukherjee,Kalika Bali,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对工具使用智能体在多语言环境下的动作策略（而非最终答案）进行了大规模测量，覆盖8个模型、6个并行基准和41种语言（238万次rollout），发现并消除了五个混淆因素后，证明语言间的行为分歧是结构性而非采样噪声；大型模型在贪心解码下能跨语言保留71-73%的动作策略，且模型身份仅解释5.7%的方差；非英语任务会经由英语路由，该枢纽具有因果承载作用；小模型（<10B参数）的表现主要受偶然下限影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言评估长期只比较最终答案而丢弃动作轨迹，但动作才是系统的实际产物，决定了成本、延迟和失败方式。现有的原始轨迹相似度测量存在五个混淆因素，每个都足以翻转结论，因此需要一种严谨的测量方法来评估工具使用智能体跨语言的行为一致性。

Method: 在8个模型、6个并行基准、41种语言上进行238万次rollout的大规模实验。识别并逐一消除五个混淆因素（短轨迹虚高、空轨迹满分、偶然一致、可复现性上限、单语言内重复不一致），然后通过置换检验、贪心解码、温度扫描、预注册预测和单一正则表达式干预等实验分离结构性与噪声性差异。

Result: 每个混淆因素的消除都使效应变大，证明分歧是结构性的而非采样噪声；四个不同的大模型在贪心解码下各自保留71-73%的动作策略，模型身份仅解释5.7%方差；小于约10B参数的模型表现瓦解，其排序主要由偶然下限决定；智能体将非英语任务路由至英语，该枢纽是因果承载的（经预注册预测验证），模型被明确告知也不放弃；一个简单的轨迹提取正则表达式就能制造多语言失败的表象。

Conclusion: 工具使用智能体的跨语言行为分歧是结构性的、可测量的，且与模型规模密切相关；大型前沿模型表现出令人惊讶的跨语言行动一致性，而小型模型的"不一致性"很大程度上是测量假象。评估动作策略而非最终答案，并充分控制混淆因素，是理解多语言工具使用行为的必要条件。

Abstract: When a tool-using agent is given the same task in a different language, does it still take the same steps? Multilingual evaluation rarely asks: it compares final answers and discards the actions. Yet those actions are the product: they fix cost and latency, decide how the system fails, and are the only auditable part of its behaviour. We make the action policy the measured object across 8 models, 6 parallel benchmarks and 41 languages (2.38M rollouts). The naive measurement fails: five confounds sit between raw trace similarity and any defensible claim, each able to flip a conclusion. Short traces score higher, empty traces score perfectly, unrelated traces agree by chance over half the time, the gap is capped by each model's reproducibility, and a model asked the same question twice in one language answers differently, leaving no baseline. We remove all five, and every correction makes the effect larger. Divergence proves structural, not sampling noise: it survives greedy decoding in every cell and stays flat as temperature rises, even as models grow less self-consistent. Normalised by their own reproducibility, four very different frontier models converge under greedy decoding, each keeping 71-73% of its action policy across languages, with model identity explaining only 5.7% of the variance. Below roughly 10B parameters it breaks down, and the ordering among smaller models is largely an artifact of a chance floor we measure by permutation rather than assume. Agents route non-English tasks through English; this pivot is causally load-bearing, confirmed by a pre-registered prediction across four models, and models will not abandon it when told to. Finally, a single trace-extraction regex, not the model, manufactured a multilingual failure: two worked examples raise one model's measured accuracy twenty-sixfold while its accuracy on readable outputs barely moves.

</details>


### [172] [Attention-Path Fragility as an Uncertainty Signal in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11138)
*Minsoo Kim,Sungyoung Ji,Kisung Moon,Ilyong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASMI（注意力子网络互信息），一种无需训练的估计器，通过掩码注意力头并测量子网络间的BALD互信息来捕捉预测的"脆弱性"，从而在不依赖输出置信度的情况下预测模型错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，模型的不确定性仅通过输出分布的广度来衡量，但本文认为，即使在置信度较高时，预测在注意力路径扰动下也可能"脆弱"。这种脆弱性包含了未被单次置信度捕获的错误预测信息，值得作为独立信号进行建模。

Method: ASMI通过掩码注意力头生成多个子网络，计算它们之间关于预测的BALD互信息，并使用语义一致性核函数来忽略表层形式的差异。此外还提出Sem-ASMI变体，可直接从单次贪婪解码中读取信号，无需随机采样生成。

Result: 在接地问答（grounded QA）任务中，ASMI在置信度过滤之外提供了额外的错误预测信息，可将置信度过滤后的残留错误减少约一半。在12个接地基准-骨干网设置中，最佳ASMI变体在8个设置中达到或超过最强基线，其中3个显著领先。Sem-ASMI在10个设置中达到或超过Semantic Entropy。在参数化QA上，所有变体按预期回落至零成本MSP基线。

Conclusion: ASMI的预测能力具有"制度分级"特征：在答案需要通过给定上下文路由的任务中表现强劲，而在依赖参数化知识回忆的任务中受限于设计边界。头级分析表明，真正跟踪这一边界的是注意力头脆弱性是否与错误耦合，而非脆弱性本身的存在。

Abstract: We propose that a model's uncertainty about a token is reflected not only in the breadth of its output distribution but also in whether a confident prediction is \emph{fragile} under perturbation of its attention pathways. We instantiate this as ASMI (Attention-Subnetwork Mutual Information), a training-free estimator that masks attention heads and measures the BALD mutual information among the resulting subnetworks, with a semantic-agreement kernel to discount surface-form disagreement. The signal is not a restatement of output confidence: on grounded QA an out-of-fold test shows it adds error-predictive information beyond single-pass confidence and entropy, concentrated in \emph{confident-but-fragile} predictions, where acting on it roughly halves the retained error of a confidence filter. The distinctness is regime-graded, so ASMI predicts its own domain of applicability, strong where answers are routed through provided context and bounded by design where they are recalled from parametric knowledge. Sem-ASMI reads the signal from a single greedy response, without the stochastic generations the strongest baselines require, and ties or beats Semantic Entropy on ten of the twelve grounded benchmark-backbone settings. Across the same twelve settings, the best ASMI variant, typically the adaptive one reusing the ten samples already drawn for the baselines, ties or leads the strongest baseline in eight, significantly in three under a paired test. On parametric QA all variants revert to or below the zero-cost MSP baseline, exactly as predicted, and the estimates are near-deterministic across reruns. A head-level analysis shows that what tracks this boundary is not the presence of head-level fragility but whether that fragility couples to errors.

</details>


### [173] [The Illusion of Cross-Lingual Safety in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11146)
*Abigail Oppong,P Sam Sahil,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Maryam Ibrahim Mukhtar,Esmael Ahmed Abdu,Tassallah Abdullahi,Jessica Oparebea,Saminu Mohammad Aliyu,Idris Abdulmumin,Abubakar Juma Chilala,Nicholaus Dismas Ladislaus,Alfred Malengo Kondoro,Lemofouet Valdini Douglace,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Seid Muhie Yimam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在多语言环境下的安全对齐问题，发现跨语言安全迁移在四种非洲低资源语言（Twi、豪萨语、阿姆哈拉语和斯瓦希里语）中严重受限。作者提出了LoDNA数据集和潜在几何分析框架，结果表明有害提示在多数语言-模型组合中保留的英文拒绝信号不足10%，说明当前多语言安全对齐是表面的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全对齐主要基于英语开发，但这种安全机制能否跨语言泛化尚未得到充分探索，特别是在低资源语言中可能存在严重漏洞。作者希望验证是否存在语言无关的通用"危害流形"这一假设。

Method: 构建LoDNA安全数据集（包含逐字翻译和文化本地化提示）；提出潜在几何分析框架，通过探测隐藏状态的拒绝表示来评估模型的安全行为，而非仅依赖生成式评估。

Result: 跨语言安全迁移严重受限，有害提示在大多数语言-模型对中保留的英文拒绝信号不足10%；逐字翻译和本地化提示语义对齐度高（余弦相似度0.95-0.996），但表示在层间漂移，说明模型编码了概念但未将其路由到安全机制。

Conclusion: 当前多语言安全对齐是表面的，研究结果强烈反对"存在通用、语言无关的危害流形"的假设（至少在这四种低资源语言中）。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is largely developed in English, assuming these safeguards generalize across multilingual settings. However, this assumption remains underexplored and exposes a vulnerability in low-resource languages. We investigate cross-lingual safety transfer in four African languages, Twi, Hausa, Amharic, and Swahili, using LoDNA, a new safety dataset that pairs literal translations with culturally localized prompts. To move beyond generation-based evaluation, we propose a latent geometric framework that probes hidden-state refusal representations in LLMs. Our experimental results show that cross-lingual safety transfer is severely limited; harmful prompts retain less than 10% of the English refusal signal across most language-model pairs. Literal and localized prompts are semantically aligned (cosine 0.95-0.996) but drift across layers, suggesting models encode the concepts without routing them to safety mechanisms. These findings demonstrate that current multilingual safety alignment is superficial, providing strong evidence against the assumption of a universal, language-agnostic harm manifold within the specific low-resource languages studied. Warning: This paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [174] [From Interpretability to Control: Insights from Six Years of the TrustNLP Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11171)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Anaelia Ovalle,Anil Ramakrishna,Anubrata Das,Apurv Verma,Jwala Dhamala,Ninareh Mehrabi,Tharindu Kumarage,Yada Pruksachatkun,Yang Trista Cao,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对2021年以来六届TrustNLP研讨会共144篇论文进行全面综述，按六个信任维度分类，揭示该领域从静态模型的事后可解释性向生成式系统的机制理解和主动控制的范式转变，并识别出真实性是增长最快的维度、公平性最稳定、可解释性呈U型轨迹等关键趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着NLP社区对信任问题的关注持续增长，TrustNLP研讨会积累了六届丰富的研究成果，但缺乏对这些论文的系统性分析和趋势总结，作者希望通过分类学框架和跨会议对比，为社区提供结构化的洞见和未来方向。

Method: 作者收集了TrustNLP全部144篇论文，基于TrustLLM和DecodingTrust等成熟框架的六个信任维度进行分类标注，并与同期ACL、NAACL、EACL、EMNLP约2000篇论文进行跨会议主题分布对比分析，同时追踪各信任维度的时间演变趋势。

Result: 研究发现：聊天模型发布激活了所有信任维度；真实性是增长最快的维度（2025-2026年占37%，2021-2022年完全缺失）；公平性是最稳定的主题；可解释性呈U型轨迹（先因事后方法失效而下降，后因机制可解释性在2026年复兴）；TrustNLP的主题分布与领域平均水平高度一致。

Conclusion: 论文识别出四个结构性洞见，并为研究社区提出了可操作的方向建议，强调信任研究正与能力涌现协同演进，未来需要更主动的控制范式来应对生成式系统的信任挑战。

Abstract: The Workshop on Trustworthy Natural Language Processing (TrustNLP), co-located with major ACL conferences since 2021, has grown from 8 proceedings papers to 41 over six editions, documenting a field-wide transition from post-hoc interpretability of static models to mechanistic understanding and proactive control of generative systems. We synthesize insights from all 144 proceedings papers, classifying them along six trust dimensions grounded in established frameworks (TrustLLM, DecodingTrust). We observe co-occurrences with capability emergence. The release of the first high-impact chat models activated all trust dimensions simultaneously, while subsequent model generations shifted focus toward truthfulness and safety alignment. Analysis from the classification study reveals that truthfulness is the fastest-growing dimension (absent in 2021-2022, comprising 37% of papers by 2025-2026), fairness remains the most consistent theme, and explainability exhibits a U-shaped trajectory; declining as post-hoc methods lost relevance but resurging in 2026 through mechanistic interpretability. A cross-venue comparison with ACL, NAACL, EACL, and EMNLP (~2K papers) in the same period shows that TrustNLP's topical distribution closely follows the field average. We identify four structural insights and conclude with actionable directions for the research community.

</details>


### [175] [ConVAWG: A Retrieval-Grounded Framework for Controlled Synthetic Dialogue Generation in Violence Against Women and Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11200)
*Chen Lyu,Xingwei Tan,Simon Cullen,Shelley Wilson,Lois Arthurs,Arshad Jhumka,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConVAWG框架，用于生成合成的、符合CPS标准的暴力侵害妇女和女孩（VAWG）多轮对话数据，通过检索增强方法构建场景、事件时间线和角色扮演对话，并发布超过6000个多轮对话事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实对话数据在敏感领域（如VAWG）难以获取、发布和标注，且现有工作多聚焦于句子级在线辱骂毒性检测，缺乏对虐待行为作为关系性和时间性展开现象的建模。

Method: ConVAWG框架包含以下步骤：从人物种子、英国国家统计局人口统计模式、官方犯罪定义和检索到的家庭凶杀案审查案例构建场景；将其转换为分层事件时间线；生成多场景角色扮演对话；并对适当的话语应用定向激活引导的毒性控制。

Result: 发布了涵盖200个场景、超过6000个多轮对话事件的数据集，包含丰富的场景级、事件级和轮次级元数据。广泛的人类评估、LLM-as-Judge评估、消融实验和下游任务显示对话质量高且领域保真度强。

Conclusion: ConVAWG成功填补了敏感领域多轮对话生成建模的空白，证明了在隐私和法律约束下合成生成高质量、领域保真的VAWG对话数据的可行性。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue generation offers a way to study conversational dynamics in sensitive domains where real data are difficult to access, release, or annotate. The underlying abuse may occur online or offline: threats and coercion can appear directly in messages, while behaviours such as surveillance, isolation, stalking, and physical violence may be planned, disclosed, or referred to conversationally. Privacy and legal constraints make it difficult the release of large-scale real conversation datasets; existing work has mostly focused on sentence-level toxicity of online abuses, leaving a gap in modelling abuse as a relational and temporally unfolding phenomenon. In this work, we focus on modelling Violence Against Women and Girls (VAWG) scenarios as multi-turn dialogues. We introduce ConVAWG, a retrieval-grounded framework for generating CPS-aligned synthetic VAWG chat dialogues. ConVAWG builds scenarios from persona seeds, demographic patterns reported by the UK Office for National Statistics, official crime definitions, and retrieved Domestic Homicide Review cases; converts them into hierarchical event timelines; generates multi-scene role-play dialogues; and applies targeted activation-steered toxicity control to appropriate utterances. We release over 6,000 multi-turn dialogue events across 200 scenarios with rich scenario-, event-, and turn-level metadata. Extensive human evaluation, LLM-as-Judge assessment, ablations, and downstream tasks show strong dialogue quality and domain fidelity.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [MD2G-Cast: Relay-Coordinated Multicast for Scalable Volumetric Streaming over MoQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10020)
*Ruonan Chai,Yisu Wang,Zili Meng,Dirk Kutscher*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出MD2G-Cast框架，利用中继协调的多播机制（基于Media over QUIC）解决体积视频流多用户传输中的重复内容浪费问题，通过PPO优化分组与增强传送选择，在100用户规模下保持P99交付间隔低于40ms，并将链路负载降低约27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积视频流传输难以扩展，因为视场重叠的接收者常被独立服务，导致共享内容被重复传输，浪费带宽资源。

Method: MD2G-Cast采用中继协调的多播框架，结合用户观看重叠度、接收端能力和带宽条件构建可复用多播组；将分组与增强传送准入建模为序列控制问题，使用PPO算法求解，并通过教师引导训练轻量级中继模型。

Result: 在20和100用户规模下，P99交付间隔均低于40ms；同构接入下达到最高或并列最高的平均系统效用，异构接入下达到最高效用；相比Clustering方法在100用户时链路负载降低约27%。消融实验表明确定性控制与PPO相当，随机动作性能下降。

Conclusion: 中继协调与选择性增强传送准入是系统的核心设计贡献，而非特定的策略优化器本身。

Abstract: Volumetric streaming remains difficult to scale because receivers with overlapping fields of view are often served independently, causing repeated transmission of shared content. We present MD2G-Cast, a relay-coordinated multicast framework over Media over QUIC with an application-aware control layer for scalable multi-user volumetric delivery. MD2G-Cast jointly uses viewing overlap, receiver capability, and bandwidth conditions to form reusable multicast groups, share common Base content, and selectively admit Enhanced delivery. We formulate grouping and Enhanced admission as a sequential control problem, realize it with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), and train a compact relay model with teacher guidance for Enhanced admission. We implement MD2G-Cast with real MoQ processes and evaluate it with real access and 6DoF viewing traces for up to 100 users. At 20 and 100 users, MD2G-Cast keeps the receiver-side $P_{99}$ delivery interval below 40 ms across all seven access profiles, while Rolling reaches the 500 ms reporting cap in most cases. Across the evaluated user scales, MD2G-Cast achieves the highest or tied-highest mean system utility under homogeneous access and the highest mean utility under heterogeneous access, while reducing aggregate link load by about 27% relative to Clustering at 100 users. A matched relay-control ablation separates the control structure from its optimizer, showing that random feasible actions reduce utility while deterministic control remains competitive with PPO. Together, the results support relay coordination and selective Enhanced admission, rather than a particular policy optimizer, as the central design contribution.

</details>


### [177] [Algorithmic statistics of retinal images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09989)
*Loan Huynh,Ronald Zambrano,Layton Aho,Fabio Lavinsky,Gadi Wollstein,Joel S. Schuman,Andrew R. Cohen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归一化压缩距离（NCD）的度量学习方法，结合各向异性结构增强滤波器，用于量化和可视化三维视网膜OCT图像中的疾病进展差异，预测误差约0.5 dB，优于非度量深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督学习方法（如神经网络）是非度量的，可能引入与生理差异无关的系统性失真；且3D视网膜OCT图像复杂难以可视化，需要一种能准确量化图像间差异并可视化疾病进展的方法。

Method: 采用归一化压缩距离（NCD）结合各向异性结构增强滤波器来度量3D视网膜图像对之间的结构差异，并提出了归一化压缩向量（NCV）作为特征集来测量和可视化图像集合中的视觉差异。

Result: NCD测量的结构差异与医生测量的视野功能变化之间的预测误差约0.5 dB，优于非度量深度学习方法。该方法在人类中度非进展性青光眼和非人灵长类动物模型（通过眼压操作）中均展示了可视化模式变化的有效性。

Conclusion: 度量学习方法（NCD+NCV）能够更准确地量化和可视化视网膜OCT图像中的疾病进展，同时通过模拟证明了非度量嵌入特征（如神经网络）可能引入类相关统计失真，凸显了度量学习的优势。

Abstract: There has been a tremendous amount of image processing and machine learning research to measure and classify disease progression from live optical coherence tomography (OCT) imaging of the retina. The images considered here are large, complex, three-dimensional (3-D) and difficult to visualize effectively. Many current supervised machine learning approaches, \emph{e.g.} neural networks, are non-metric meaning that any features or measurements generated can introduce systematic distortion that may be correlated with underlying non-meaningful physiological differences. Here we present a metric learning approach using the normalized compression distance (NCD) combined with anisotropic structure-enhancing filters to quantify and visualize the principal differences among a collection of 3-D retinal images. We validate the NCD-measured structural differences between pairs of images against the physician-measured change in visual field function, achieving a prediction error of $\sim$ 0.5 dB, more accurate than non-metric deep learning approaches. The normalized compression vectors (NCV) are proposed as a feature set measuring visual differences among a collection of 3-D microscopy images. The utility of the NCV for visualizing and measuring patterns of change is demonstrated for a human with moderate non-progressing glaucoma and for a non-human primate model using intraocular pressure setting manipulation. We conclude with a brief simulation of non-metric embedding features, \emph{e.g.} from neural networks, introducing class-correlated statistical distortion.

</details>


### [178] [Longitudinal 3D Foundation Modeling for Neoadjuvant Breast Cancer Response Prediction from Serial DCE-MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09991)
*Fidel Omar Tito Cruz,Neda Ghafouri,Zengyan Wang,Pegah Khosravi,Yu Tian,Chen Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种纵向框架，结合冻结的3D基础编码器（Pillar-0）和时序动力学网络（TDN），利用四个临床时间点的DCE-MRI序列预测乳腺癌新辅助化疗后的病理完全缓解（pCR）。在982例I-SPY2和ACRIN-6698联合队列上，当纵向3D影像与临床数据融合时，模型取得AUROC 73.6%、平衡准确率69.1%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pCR是乳腺癌新辅助化疗的重要预后指标，但现有影像学方法多依赖单一时点成像，无法捕捉治疗过程中肿瘤的动态变化，从而限制了治疗反应预测的准确性。

Method: 构建纵向预测框架：使用冻结的3D基础编码器Pillar-0提取影像特征，结合提出的时序动力学网络（TDN）整合时间感知的体素级嵌入，并融合临床与治疗数据；输入为跨预处理至术前四个临床时间点的动态对比增强MRI序列。

Result: 在982例来自I-SPY2和ACRIN-6698联合队列的患者上，融合纵向3D影像与临床数据时取得了最佳性能（测试AUROC 73.6%，平衡准确率69.1%）；临床变量单独提供最强的预测信号，但纵向3D影像在融合时提供了互补信息。

Conclusion: 纵向多时点3D影像与临床数据融合可有效改善pCR预测性能，两者具有互补性；该框架为治疗反应评估提供了可行方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Pathologic complete response (pCR) is an important endpoint in neoadjuvant chemotherapy (NAC) for breast cancer, and predicting pCR from imaging during treatment could support treatment response assessment. Many existing imaging-based approaches rely on a single static timepoint, which fails to capture changes that occur during treatment. In this work, we present a longitudinal framework that combines a frozen 3D foundation encoder (Pillar-0) with our Temporal Dynamics Network (TDN) to predict treatment response from serial Dynamic Contrast-Enhanced (DCE) MRI acquired across four clinical timepoints from pre-treatment to pre-surgery. The TDN combines time-aware volumetric embeddings with clinical and treatment data to predict pCR. Evaluated on 982 patients from the combined I-SPY2 and ACRIN-6698 cohort, the proposed model achieves strong performance across all reported metrics when longitudinal 3D imaging is fused with clinical data (test AUROC: 73.6%, balanced accuracy: 69.1%). While clinical variables provide the strongest individual predictive signal, longitudinal 3D imaging contributes complementary information when fused with clinical data, improving pCR prediction. Our source code is available at: https://github.com/omarftt/longitudinal_temporal_pillar.

</details>


### [179] [Knowledge-Guided 3D CT Generation: A Conditioning-Centric Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09992)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Eugenio Lomurno,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以条件控制为中心的3D CT生成分类体系，通过"外部知识类型 × 知识集成范式 × 生成架构"三个正交维度构建设计空间，用于系统化梳理和比较现有条件3D CT生成方法，识别主流趋势并指出未充分探索的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部知识引导的条件生成在3D CT领域对临床数据增强、隐私保护数据共享和病理场景模拟至关重要。然而，该领域研究快速增长但方法多样性导致系统比较困难，缺乏统一框架来理解基本设计选择。

Method: 提出一个以条件控制为中心的分类体系（taxonomy），沿三个正交维度组织文献：外部知识类型(K)、知识集成范式(I)、生成架构(A)。通过K × I × A因式分解定义显式设计空间，提供统一视角来审视已有工作。

Result: 利用该框架系统化了现有方法，识别出主导趋势和反复出现的设计模式，并指出了设计空间中探索不足的区域，为未来研究提供了有前景的方向。

Conclusion: 该分类体系为3D CT条件生成领域提供了结构化的分析工具，有助于研究者理解领域全貌、识别研究空白，并指导未来研究方向的探索。

Abstract: Controllable generation guided by external knowledge is a key requirement in modern generative deep learning applications, enabling the synthesis of samples with explicit constraints on semantic content, structural properties, and variability. In 3D Computed Tomography (CT), such control is essential for clinical applications, including data augmentation, privacy-preserving data sharing, and the simulation of specific anatomical or pathological scenarios. While research on conditional 3D CT generation has expanded rapidly, the diversity of existing approaches makes systematic comparison difficult and obscures fundamental design choices. In this survey, we propose a conditioning-centric taxonomy that organizes the literature along three orthogonal dimensions: the type of external knowledge (K), the knowledge integration paradigm (I), and the generative architecture (A). This factorization defines an explicit design space (K x I x A) that provides a unified perspective on prior work. Using this framework, we systematize existing methods, identify dominant trends and recurring design patterns, and highlight underexplored regions of the design space that point toward promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [180] [APCReg: Anatomical-Prior-Guided Coarse-to-Fine CBCT--IOS Registration via Multi-View Projection and Reliability-Controlled Residual Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09993)
*Xincan Zheng,Yaqi Wang,Zhi Li,Jiahao Bao,Lan Feng,Yiru Xia,Shuai Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出APCReg，一个基于解剖先验引导的粗到细框架，用于CBCT与口内扫描（IOS）之间的全局配准和可靠性控制的残差校正，在60对测试颌骨上实现了亚毫米级的平均Chamfer距离（0.87mm）和Hausdorff距离（2.92mm），并在六项评估指标中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBCT与IOS之间的配准对个性化手术规划至关重要，但现有自动配准方法因模态差异大、重叠区域有限和位姿偏移大而不可靠，临床实践中仍依赖传统几何流程和手动调整，亟需一种可靠的自动化配准方案。

Method: 提出APCReg框架，包含三个核心模块：（1）多视角解剖粗配准（MACR），通过有序正交投影对齐（颊侧、近中、咬合）将六自由度搜索分解后再进行三维精化；（2）重叠感知残差配准（OARR），融合共享KPConv特征、折叠弓长线索、重叠门控交叉注意力和Sinkhorn匹配；（3）牙弓结构假设选择，在保留的可靠对应点上评估多种位姿，并通过无真值粗保留守卫条件性地保留几何上可靠的粗位姿。

Result: 在60对未见过的颌骨测试对上，APCReg实现了0.87mm的平均Chamfer距离和2.92mm的Hausdorff距离，达到亚毫米精度，并在所有评估的开源基线方法的六项报告指标中排名第一。

Conclusion: APCReg通过解剖先验引导的粗到细策略，有效解决了CBCT与IOS跨模态配准中模态差异大、重叠有限及大位姿偏移等难题，为临床应用提供了高精度且可靠的自动化配准方案。

Abstract: Registration between cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) and intraoral scans (IOS) is essential for patient-specific surgical planning. However, disparate imaging modalities, limited overlap, and large pose offsets make automated registration unreliable. Consequently, clinical registration remains dependent on conventional geometry pipelines and manual clinician adjustment. To address these challenges, we propose APCReg, an anatomical-prior-guided coarse-to-fine framework for global registration and reliability-controlled residual correction. Specifically, multi-view anatomical coarse registration (MACR) performs ordered orthogonal projection alignment (buccal, proximal, and occlusal) to decompose the six-degree-of-freedom search before three-dimensional refinement. Overlap-aware residual registration (OARR) combines shared KPConv features, a folded arch-length cue, overlap-gated cross-attention, and Sinkhorn matching. Finally, dental-arch-structured hypothesis selection evaluates diverse poses on held-out reliable correspondences, while a ground-truth-free coarse-retention guard conditionally retains a geometrically reliable coarse pose. On 60 held-out jaw pairs, APCReg achieves a submillimeter mean Chamfer distance of 0.87 mm and a Hausdorff distance of 2.92 mm under this evaluation protocol, and ranks first across the six reported metrics among the evaluated open-source baselines.

</details>


### [181] [SpecF2M: A Spectral-Aware Multi-task Network Estimating Axial Length and Refractive Error from Pediatric Fundus Photographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09994)
*Mengxian He,Xinyue Liu,Yunyun Sun,Wei Hao,Minqing Zhang,Lichun Wang,Shunyi Zhang,Wu Yuan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecF2M，一种频谱感知的多任务网络，用于从儿童眼底照片中估计屈光状态（AL和SER分量）。在4,359名儿童、6,966张眼底图像上，AL的MAE达0.5347 mm，SPH的MAE达0.7062 D，优于CNN/ViT基线，并揭示CYL与眼底近视模式关联较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童近视筛查中，SER和AL是关键指标，但测量需要专用生物测量仪和睫状肌麻痹验光，成本高且不便。眼底摄影作为一种易获取的成像方式，虽能反映近视相关的后极部变化，但这些线索通常是低对比度、空间分散且多尺度的。此外，AL、SPH和CYL共享部分重叠但不完全相同的解剖相关性，需要能捕捉这些复杂关系的建模方法。

Method: SpecF2M包含三个核心组件：(1)解剖引导的确定性增强模块，用于增强低对比度的眼底近视相关线索；(2)混合空间-频谱骨干网络，结合MixCNN和混合频谱学习（HSL）块，捕捉多尺度和多模态特征；(3)专家路由头，用于组件级别的AL、SPH和CYL估计，处理三者之间部分重叠但不完全一致的任务耦合关系。

Result: 在包含4,359次儿童就诊和6,966张眼底图像的数据集上，SpecF2M在AL和SPH估计上优于受控的CNN/ViT基线，分别达到0.5347 mm和0.7062 D的MAE。组件级别分析揭示了不对称的任务耦合，CYL与眼底衍生的近视模式关联弱于AL/SPH。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于眼底图像的儿童近视指标估计具有可行性和筛查价值。CYL与眼底模式关联较弱这一发现为理解不同屈光分量的眼部解剖基础提供了新见解。然而，在部署之前仍需要外部验证来确认泛化性能。

Abstract: Spherical Equivalent Refraction (SER) and Axial Length (AL) are core indicators for pediatric myopia screening, yet their measurements require dedicated biometry and cycloplegic refraction. Fundus photography offers an accessible imaging modality, as myopia-related posterior-pole changes are visible in 45$^\circ$ fundus images. However, these cues are often low-contrast, spatially diffuse, and multi-scale. Moreover, AL, Sphere (SPH), and Cylinder (CYL) share partially overlapping but non-identical anatomical correlates. We propose SpecF2M, a spectral-aware multi-task network for estimating AL and SER components from pediatric fundus photographs. SpecF2M integrates a deterministic anatomy-guided enhancement module, a hybrid spatial--spectral backbone combining MixCNN and Hybrid Spectral Learning (HSL) blocks, and an expert-routing head for component-level estimation of AL, SPH, and CYL. On a pediatric cohort of 4,359 eligible child visits and 6,966 fundus images, SpecF2M outperforms controlled CNN/ViT baselines for AL and SPH estimation, achieving MAEs of 0.5347 mm and 0.7062 D, respectively. Component-level analysis further reveals asymmetric task coupling, where CYL exhibits weaker association with fundus-derived myopic patterns than AL/SPH. These results support fundus-based, screening-oriented estimation of pediatric myopia indicators, while external validation remains necessary before deployment.

</details>


### [182] [Structural Guidance for Unified Joint Demosaicing and Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09995)
*Qixin Zheng,Ping Chen,Qiangqiang Shen,Haijin Zeng*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结构引导的统一去马赛克与去噪框架，通过注入预训练结构先验（伪RGB观测）来增强CFA感知的图像恢复，并引入轻量级适配器弥合传感器域与自然图像域之间的差距，在多个CFA模式和噪声水平下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一恢复网络虽然显式建模了CFA几何结构，但主要由像素级监督驱动，在边缘、重复纹理和摩尔纹等局部证据不可靠的区域容易出现结构退化。作者认为根本原因在于缺乏像素级重建监督之外的结构引导。

Method: 该框架包括：(1) 统一的五通道观测输入（原始马赛克、CFA掩码、噪声水平图）；(2) 基于SwinIR的恢复分支在CFA条件调制下重建像素细节；(3) 并行的结构推理分支从稀疏伪RGB观测中提取互补结构线索；(4) 轻量级可训练适配器弥合稀疏噪声传感器数据与自然图像预训练域之间的领域差距；(5) 残差融合结构和恢复特征；(6) 共享解码器联合预测恢复RGB图像和辅助清洁马赛克，提供图像域和传感器域的双重监督。

Result: 在多种CFA模式和噪声水平下的大量实验表明，该方法在统一方法和CFA特定方法上均取得一致性改进，验证了适配的结构先验能增强鲁棒的相机图像恢复。

Conclusion: 适配的结构先验可以有效弥补像素级监督的不足，提升统一去马赛克与去噪框架在复杂结构区域的恢复鲁棒性，代码和数据集已在补充材料中提供。

Abstract: Joint demosaicing and denoising is a fundamental step in camera image signal processing, yet remains challenging because different Bayer-like color filter arrays (CFAs) and sensor noise jointly corrupt both color sampling and image content. Existing unified restoration networks explicitly model CFA geometry but are still driven primarily by pixel-level supervision, making them prone to structural degradation around edges, repetitive textures, and moiré patterns where local evidence is unreliable. We attribute this limitation partly to the absence of explicit structural guidance beyond pixel-level reconstruction supervision. Motivated by this observation, we propose a structural-guided unified restoration framework that injects pretrained structural knowledge into CFA-aware image restoration. Our model receives a unified five-channel observation consisting of the raw mosaic, CFA masks, and a noise-level map. A SwinIR restoration branch reconstructs pixel details under CFA-conditioned modulation, while a parallel structural reasoning branch extracts complementary structural cues from a sparse pseudo-RGB observation. To bridge the substantial domain gap between sparse noisy sensor data and the natural-image pretraining domain of the structural encoder, we introduce a lightweight trainable adapter before residually fusing structural and restoration features. A shared decoder jointly predicts the restored RGB image and an auxiliary clean mosaic, providing supervision in both image and sensor domains. Extensive experiments across multiple CFA patterns and noise levels demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art unified and CFA-specific methods, indicating that adapted structural priors can enhance robust camera image restoration. The source codes and dataset are provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [183] [Energy and Performance Benchmarking of Deep Learning Models for Breast Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09996)
*Samar Garrab,Ghada Achour*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文对七种深度学习模型在两个医学数据集（乳腺超声和BreakHis 400X）上的乳腺癌检测性能及CO2排放进行了比较分析，结果表明EfficientNet和ResNet性能优异但排放较高，DeiT在超声数据集上取得最佳性能与能耗平衡，而没有任何单一架构能在两个数据集上始终占优，强调了模型选择需综合考虑性能、排放和数据集特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型在医学图像分析中的应用日益广泛且架构日趋复杂，其环境足迹正成为日益突出的问题。目前缺乏对乳腺癌检测模型中性能与环境影响之间权衡的系统性研究。

Method: 在两个医学数据集（Breast Ultrasound和BreakHis 400X）上对七种深度学习模型进行比较分析，涵盖CNN、Transformer和混合架构；除评估性能指标外，还分别测量了训练和推理阶段的CO2排放。

Result: EfficientNet和ResNet始终表现优异但CO2排放较高；DeiT-Tiny等Transformer在两个数据集上表现具有竞争力；DenseNet121准确率较低。在乳腺超声数据集上DeiT实现了最佳的精度与能耗平衡，而在BreakHis数据集上ViT和Swin模型表现最佳。

Conclusion: 所选架构类别中没有任何单一模型能在两个数据上持续占据主导地位，表明在医学应用中应联合考虑性能、排放和数据集特征来选择合适的模型。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have greatly improved breast cancer detection, enabling more accurate and timely diagnosis. Deep learning (DL) models show strong potential for medical image analysis; however, as their architectural complexity increases, their environmental impacts are becoming a growing concern. In this paper, we present a comparative analysis of seven DL models for breast cancer detection on two medical datasets: Breast Ultrasound and BreakHis 400X. The evaluated architectures range from Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and transformers to hybrid models. In addition to performance metrics, we assess CO2 emissions during both training and inference. Our results show that EfficientNet and ResNet consistently deliver strong performance, although with higher CO2 emissions. The selected transformers, such as DeiT-Tiny, perform competitively on both datasets, whereas DenseNet121 achieves lower accuracy. On the Breast Ultrasound Dataset, DeiT provides the most favourable balance between accuracy and energy consumption, whereas on the BreakHis dataset, the ViT and Swin models achieve the best results. Overall, our findings indicate that no single architecture category from the evaluated ones consistently dominates across the two selected datasets. Our results highlight the importance of jointly considering performance, emissions, and dataset characteristics when selecting models for medical applications.

</details>


### [184] [Robustness of transferability estimation metrics for medical imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09999)
*Niclas Claßen,Théo Sourget,Dovile Juodelyte,Rob van der Goot,Veronika Cheplygina*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文研究了迁移学习中源模型选择问题，重点关注可迁移性估计（TE）指标在医学影像领域的稳健性。通过构建不同样本量和随机种子的微型数据集，并改变评估指标，发现目标数据集的微小变化和评估指标的选择都会显著影响TE指标的排名结果，整体上TE指标与参考排名的一致性较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学影像迁移学习中，选择预训练源模型至关重要，但现有可迁移性估计（TE）指标在不同实验设置下报告了矛盾的结果，且大多针对自然图像设计、以准确率为优化目标，未充分考虑医学影像中类别不平衡等特殊问题。因此需要系统研究TE指标在医学影像场景下的稳健性。

Method: 作者将目标数据集的变化作为隔离因素进行研究，构建了不同样本量和随机种子的微型数据集种群。同时，研究了用于获取参考排名的评估指标（如准确率与更稳健的医学影像指标）对TE指标评价结果的影响。

Result: 研究发现：（1）目标数据集的微小修改（如样本量和随机种子变化）会改变源模型的排名；（2）评估指标的选择会影响参考排名，从而影响TE指标的评估结果；（3）TE指标与参考排名之间的总体一致性较低。

Conclusion: TE指标在医学影像场景下的稳健性存疑，目标数据集的变化和评估指标的选择都会对排名结果产生显著影响。研究提供了开源代码、模型检查点和数据划分，便于后续研究者复现和深入探索。

Abstract: In transfer learning, the choice of source model largely influences the performance on a target dataset. Still, selecting a fitting source remains a challenging task, especially in medical imaging where one has to decide between models pre-trained on off-the-shelf options, such as ImageNet, and domain specific datasets. Transferability estimation (TE) metrics address this problem by aiming to predict the best performing source model in a computationally cost effective way. However, previous work has reported conflicting TE metric performances due to differences in experimental setups. Moreover, most TE metrics are designed for and evaluated on natural images, while being optimized for accuracy, whereas in medical imaging metrics that are more robust to class imbalance are typically used. We study the impact of varying the target dataset as an isolated factor, by constructing miniature populations of different sample sizes and random seeds. In addition, we investigate the influence of the evaluation metric used to obtain the reference ranking. We find that small modifications to the target dataset change the rankings. Furthermore, we show that the choice of evaluation metric affects the reference rankings and therefore the evaluation of TE metrics. Overall, we observe a low agreement between rankings from TE metrics and reference. The code, model checkpoints and data splits used in this work are available through https://github.com/niclasclassen/robustness-of-transferability-estimation-metrics-for-medical-imaging.

</details>


### [185] [Pre- to Post-Contrast Synthesis of Breast DCE-MRI using Latent Bridge Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10000)
*Sina Amirrajab,Zohaib Salahuddin,Henry C Woodruff,Philippe Lambin*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜空间桥匹配（LBM）的框架，用于从增强前乳腺MRI合成峰值增强图像，与传统的潜扩散模型不同，该方法在配对的增强前和峰值增强VAE潜变量之间学习条件桥，并通过肿瘤掩膜条件化进一步提升性能，在DUKE验证集上优于LDM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DCE-MRI是乳腺癌成像的核心技术，但钆造影剂的使用增加了扫描负担，推动了减少造影剂的替代方案（如合成对比增强）的需求。传统潜扩散模型从高斯噪声出发生成图像，无法保证生成结果与患者特定解剖结构对齐，因此需要一种以增强前图像为锚点的生成框架。

Method: 提出潜桥匹配（LBM）框架：不是在潜扩散模型那样从高斯噪声出发，而是在配对的增强前与峰值增强VAE潜变量之间学习条件桥。潜UNet从中间桥状态预测到达峰值增强潜变量所需的剩余修正，实现迭代细化。评估了两种条件化变体：增强前图像条件化和肿瘤掩膜条件化。

Result: 在91个DUKE验证病例上，肿瘤条件化LBM相比增强前条件化表现更优，MSE从1.023降至0.940，FRD从7.523降至4.716，肿瘤区域SSIM从0.355提升至0.429。肿瘤条件化LBM也优于所评估的LDM基线。

Conclusion: 潜桥匹配是以增强前图像为锚点的虚拟对比增强的有前景方法，但需要进一步工作验证泛化能力，并在推理时消除对真实肿瘤掩膜的依赖。

Abstract: Dynamic contrast-enhanced magnetic resonance imaging (DCE-MRI) is central to breast cancer imaging, but gadolinium administration increases scan burden and motivates contrast-reduced alternatives, including synthetic contrast generation. We propose a latent bridge matching (LBM) framework for synthesizing peak-enhanced breast DCE-MRI from pre-contrast images in the MAMA-SYNTH challenge setting. Instead of starting from Gaussian noise as in conventional latent diffusion models (LDMs), the proposed model learns a conditional bridge between paired pre-contrast and peak-enhanced VAE latents. A latent UNet predicts the remaining correction from intermediate bridge states to the peak-enhanced latent, enabling iterative refinement while keeping the trajectory anchored to patient-specific anatomy. We evaluated two LBM conditioning variants on 91 DUKE validation cases. For the tumor-conditioned variant, tumor masks were used as conditioning inputs. Tumor-conditioning improved performance compared with pre-contrast conditioning, reducing MSE from 1.023 to 0.940 and FRD from 7.523 to 4.716, while increasing tumor SSIM from 0.355 to 0.429. The tumor-conditioned LBM also outperformed the evaluated LDM baseline on this validation cohort. These results suggest that latent bridge matching is a promising pre-contrast-anchored formulation for virtual contrast enhancement, while further work is needed to validate generalization and remove dependence on ground-truth tumor masks at inference.

</details>


### [186] [Implicit representations are dead. Long live explicit primitives!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10001)
*Nil Stolt-Ansó,Maik Dannecker,Wenqi Huang,Andras Jakab,Daniel Rueckert*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文对高斯显式表示与隐式神经表示在医学影像中的性能进行了跨维度综合评估，证明前者在重建质量、优化时间和内存需求上均优于或匹敌后者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式神经表示虽具有高保真度和紧凑存储优势，但其依赖全局多层感知机导致计算开销大、内存占用高、优化时间长，难以满足医学影像向高分辨率体积发展的需求。

Method: 首先从理论上概述了显式高斯基元相比隐式神经范式所提供的数学性质优势；随后在2D显微组织学图像和3D肺部CT两个数据集上进行计算性能基准测试。

Result: 实验表明，在所有压缩因子下，高斯表示的重建指标均达到或超过隐式方法，同时表现出显著更低的优化时间和内存需求。

Conclusion: 高斯表示凭借其优异的性能表现和数学性质，有望成为医学影像隐式表示的替代方案，值得在未来的医学影像研究中更广泛地采用。

Abstract: Continuous parameterization of medical data has emerged as a powerful paradigm for resolution-independent image representation. While Implicit Neural Representations offer high fidelity and compact storage, their reliance on global Multi-Layer Perceptrons incurs sizeable computational costs, large memory requirements, and extensive optimization times. As medical imaging trends towards ever-more detailed, high-resolution volumes, these costs impose significant bottlenecks in the applicability of implicit approaches. Recently, explicit Gaussian-based primitives have revolutionized the representation learning paradigm by trading deep network evaluations for localized, rasterization-friendly primitives. In this paper, we present a comprehensive, cross-dimensional evaluation of Gaussian representations against implicit approaches for medical imaging applications. First, we outline a theoretical overview on the mathematical properties offered by explicit primitives beyond what is capable under the implicit neural paradigm. Subsequently, we benchmark the computational performance on two demanding image datasets: 2D microscopy histology and 3D lung Computed Tomography (CT). Our experiments demonstrate that Gaussian representations consistently match or surpass reconstruction metrics compared to implicit methods across all compression factors, while displaying significantly lower optimization times, and memory requirements. Together with the compelling mathematical properties offered by explicit primitives, these findings motivate the wider adoption of Gaussian representations and position them as an attractive direction for future research in medical imaging.

</details>


### [187] [LoRCA: LoRA Cycle Adaptation for Histology to HiP-CT Translation with DINOv3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10002)
*Yang Zhou,Edoardo Occhipinti,Banboye Kidzeru Elvis,Jishizhan Chen,Stathis Megas,Joseph Brunet,Joanna Purzycka,Theresa Urban,Hector Dejea,Sarah Amalia Teichmann,Menna R Clatworthy,Paul Tafforeau,Peter D Lee,Claire L Walsh*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: LoRCA是一种基于LoRA循环适应的无监督风格翻译框架，用于将H&E组织学图像翻译为HiP-CT体积图像，以辅助非线性配准。该方法利用冻结的DINOv3骨干网络和模态特异LoRA适配器，在不需配对数据的情况下实现结构保持的样式转换，在翻译质量和结构一致性上优于CycleGAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HiP-CT成像技术为组织学提供了三维器官级别的背景信息，但组织学与HiP-CT之间的非线性配准因不同色彩空间的特征表示差异而极具挑战。现有的合成-再配准方法要么依赖手工解剖轮廓，要么在无语义约束下从头训练，限制了对软组织器官和新模态的泛化能力。

Method: 提出LoRCA框架：基于循环一致性风格翻译，采用共享的冻结DINOv3骨干网络，搭配模态特异的LoRA适配器，学习模态特异表示并通过解码器和对抗训练实现结构保持的风格翻译。冻结骨干作为结构锚点，防止内容漂移并保留预训练语义提取能力，无需配对训练数据。

Result: 使用FID评估翻译质量、互信息和Canny边缘保留评估结构保真度。LoRCA在翻译质量和结构一致性上均优于CycleGAN。在手动对齐的HiP-CT和组织学测试对上，风格翻译后的图像在MatchAnything下产生更多特征对应点。

Conclusion: LoRCA风格翻译是2D组织学切片到3D HiP-CT体积配准的有前景的第一步，展示了冻结语义骨干结合LoRA适配器在不需配对数据的情况下实现高质量结构保持翻译的潜力。

Abstract: Hierarchical Phase-Contrast Tomography (HiP-CT) is a synchrotron based X-ray imaging technique that enables non-destructive, volumetric imaging of intact organs with multi-resolutions bridging 20 $μm$/voxel for whole organs to near-cellular resolution ($\sim$0.8 $μm$/voxel) in local regions. This offers the opportunity to bring volumetric whole-organ context to histology. However, nonlinear registration between H\&E histology and HiP-CT volumes is challenging due to the differences in feature representations of different colour spaces. Synthesis-before-registration methods have shown strong results in histology-to-MRI and histology-to-CT alignment. However, existing approaches either rely on manual anatomical contours or are trained from scratch without semantic constraints, limiting their generalisability to soft tissue organs and novel modalities. We propose LoRCA (LoRA Cycle Adaptation), a cycle consistent style translation framework built on a shared frozen DINOv3 with modality-specific LoRA adapters, learning modality-specific representations that are decoded and adversarially trained. LoRCA enables structure-preserving translation without requiring paired training data. The frozen backbone is intended to be a structural anchor that prevents content drift by preserving pretrained semantic-extraction capability. We evaluate translation quality using Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and structural fidelity via mutual information and Canny edge preservation. LoRCA outperforms CycleGAN in both translation quality and structural consistency. As a preliminary indicator of downstream registration utility, we find that style-translated images yield increased feature correspondences under MatchAnything on manually aligned HiP-CT and histology test pairs, suggesting that LoRCA-style translation is a promising step towards 2D histological sections to 3D HiP-CT volumes registration.

</details>


### [188] [When Repository Labels Are Not Image-Level Truth: A Supervision Auditing Framework for Chest Radiograph AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10084)
*Yesika Alexandra Agudelo-Londoño,Jhon Wilmer Pino-Román,Brahian Carrera Rodríguez,José Miguel Castañeda-Bedoya,Juan Pablo Gómez-López,Aura C. Puche-Sarmiento,Niharika S. D'Souza,Juan Sebastian Osorio-Valencia,Jon E. Duque-Grajales,Jazmín Ximena Suárez-Revelo,Jorge Mario Vélez-Arango,Gabriel Castrillón*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出存储库监督审计（RSA）框架，用于在模型开发前评估从放射学报告中提取的存储库标签与专家图像级标注的一致性。以MIMIC-CXR中的心脏肥大为例，研究发现存储库标签与专家图像级评估几乎无一致性，仅识别出1%的专家确认病例，其中近半数"无发现"标签的研究实际存在心脏肥大。基于专家标注队列训练的DenseNet121模型ROC-AUC达0.853。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共胸部X光存储库的标签通常从放射学报告中提取，而非直接在图像上验证，这导致标签可能无法真实反映图像中可见的病理特征。现有研究往往将这些标签直接视为图像级金标准，缺乏对标签可靠性的系统验证，可能影响医学AI系统的可信度。

Method: 提出RSA（Repository Supervision Auditing）框架，以MIMIC-CXR中的心脏肥大作为案例研究。该框架将存储库衍生标签与放射科医生审查的图像级标注进行比较，表征分歧来源（如非提及vs明确否定），并构建专家整理的队列用于面向部署的模型评估。

Result: 存储库心脏肥大标签与专家图像级评估几乎无一致性，仅识别出1%的专家确认病例。大多数分歧源于报告中的"非提及"而非明确否定。近半数被分配"No Finding"标签的研究中实际存在专家确认的心脏肥大。基于专家整理队列训练的DenseNet121测试ROC-AUC为0.853。

Conclusion: 存储库标签可能无法可靠代表图像级真实情况，监督审计是开发可信医学影像AI的关键前置步骤。该研究强调在模型开发前验证数据标签可靠性的重要性。

Abstract: Public chest X-ray repositories are widely used to train medical AI systems, yet their labels are typically extracted from radiology reports rather than verified directly on images. As a result, repository labels are often treated as image-level ground truth without validating whether they reflect what is actually visible in the radiograph. We introduce Repository Supervision Auditing (RSA), a framework that evaluates repository-derived labels against expert image-level annotations before model development. Using cardiomegaly in MIMIC-CXR as a case study, RSA compares repository labels with radiologist-reviewed image annotations, characterizes disagreement sources, and builds a curated cohort for deployment-oriented evaluation. Repository-derived cardiomegaly labels showed near-zero agreement with expert image-level assessment, identifying only 1% of expert-confirmed cases. Most discrepancies resulted from non-mention rather than explicit report negation, with expert-confirmed cardiomegaly identified in nearly half of studies assigned a repository-derived No Finding label. Using the resulting expert-curated cohort, a DenseNet121 model achieved a test ROC-AUC of 0.853. These findings show that repository labels may not reliably represent image-level truth and highlight supervision auditing as a critical step for developing trustworthy medical imaging AI.

</details>


### [189] [BreastMammo and DenseMammo: Benchmarks for Mammography Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10271)
*Hongyi Pan,Gorkem Durak,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Andrea Mia Bejar,Mustafa Ege Seker,Nebile Alibeyoglu,Rumeysa Guclu,Rana Gunoz Comert Bozkurt,Sibel Ozkan Gurdal,Neslihan Cabioglu,Beyza Ozcinar,Ravza Yilmaz,Vahit Ozmen,Erkin Aribal,Sukru Mehmet Erturk,Yalda Zafari,Mohamed Mabrok,Kayhan Batmanghelich,Mohammad Yaqub,Ziyue Xu,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于前景直方图匹配的域泛化框架，并引入两个新的乳腺X光数据集（BreastMammo和DenseMammo），以解决乳腺密度分类中跨临床站点、跨设备导致的域偏移问题。Swin Transformer骨干在内部5折交叉验证中达到98.32%的AUC，外部数据集上显著优于MixStyle和DFT-based等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺密度分类是乳腺癌风险评估的关键，但AI模型常因不同厂商设备的采集风格差异（域偏移）而难以跨临床站点泛化，亟需鲁棒的域泛化解决方案。

Method: 引入两个新数据集BreastMammo和DenseMammo；提出仅针对前景的直方图匹配（foreground-only histogram matching）协议来消除来自不同临床来源的域偏移；使用Swin Transformer作为骨干网络。

Result: 内部5折交叉验证中，Swin Transformer骨干达到峰值AUC 98.32%；在外部TNMammo和LUMINA数据集上，该方法持续减少域偏移，显著优于MixStyle和基于离散傅里叶变换（DFT）的域泛化方法。

Conclusion: 提出的前景直方图匹配域泛化框架能够有效缓解多中心乳腺密度分类中的域偏移问题，提升模型在未见临床环境下的泛化能力，并证明新数据集对该领域研究具有重要价值。

Abstract: Breast density classification is a critical component of breast cancer risk assessment, yet AI models often struggle to generalize across clinical sites due to vendor-specific acquisition styles. In this work, we introduce two new datasets, BreastMammo and DenseMammo, to facilitate robust multi-view mammography research. We propose a domain generalization framework that utilizes a foreground-only histogram matching protocol to resolve the domain shift issue arising from disparate clinical sources. Internal evaluation using a 5-fold cross-validation protocol demonstrates the efficacy of our approach, with the Swin Transformer backbone achieving a peak AUC of 98.32% for density classification. External evaluation on the TNMammo and LUMINA datasets demonstrates that the proposed approach consistently reduces domain shift, significantly outperforming prominent domain generalization paradigms, including MixStyle and Discrete-Fourier-Transform-based frameworks.

</details>


### [190] [Retrieval-Augmented Vision Foundation Models for Robust Leukemia Cell Classification across Multiple Microscopy Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10657)
*Carlos Zamora,Hiram Zuniga,Ulises Orozco-Rosas,Kenia Picos*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段白血病细胞图像分类框架，使用预训练视觉基础模型，在跨五个异构数据集上实现二分类（白血病/非白血病）和亚型分类（ALL/AML），并通过留出数据集协议评估域漂移泛化能力，同时对比三种编码器和多种适应方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白血病细胞图像分类面临真实世界中来自采集、染色、光照和站点协议等域漂移挑战，单数据集模型在真实临床场景中泛化能力差。

Method: 两阶段pipeline：第一阶段用122,167张单细胞图像做二分类，第二阶段对阳性样本用69,400张图像做ALL/AML亚型分类。跨五个异构数据集进行标签协调，采用留出数据集协议评估泛化。基准测试三种编码器（DinoBloom、BiomedCLIP、CLIP），使用线性探测、LoRA和检索增强分类（RAC）模块。

Result: 研究量化了域专属预训练在域漂移下的贡献，并评估了低成本适应和检索方法是否可作为昂贵域专属预训练的可行替代方案。留出协议还能诊断性能是否归因于数据集特定伪影而非细胞形态学特征。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个稳健的白血病分类框架，同时通过留出协议揭示分类性能的归因来源，为临床场景下的跨数据集泛化提供了实用方案和诊断工具。

Abstract: Leukemia cell image classification is challenged by real-world domain shifts from acquisition, staining, illumination, and site protocols, causing single-dataset models to generalize poorly in real clinical scenarios. This work presents a robust framework for leukemia classification across multiple heterogeneous datasets using a two-stage pipeline with a pretrained vision foundation model. Stage 1 performs binary classification (leukemia vs. non-leukemia) and is trained using 122,167 single-cell images. Stage 2 is conditionally applied to Stage 1 positives to perform subtype classification into Acute Lymphoblastic Leukemia (ALL) and Acute Myeloid Leukemia (AML), trained using 69,400 single-cell images. Labels are harmonized across five heterogeneous datasets to enable cross-dataset training, and performance is evaluated on a held-out dataset protocol to assess domain-shift generalization. Within this pipeline, three encoders are benchmarked (DinoBloom, pretrained on single-cell images; BiomedCLIP, pretrained on biomedical data; and CLIP as a general-purpose model) under linear probing, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), and a Retrieval-Augmented Classification (RAC) module that retrieves the top-k most similar cell images to provide cytomorphological grounding. The objective is to quantify how much domain-specific pretraining contributes to performance under domain shift, and whether cost-effective adaptation and retrieval can be a viable alternative to expensive domain-specialized pretraining. The held-out protocol additionally serves as a diagnostic tool, revealing when classification performance is attributable to dataset-specific artifacts rather than to cytomorphological features.

</details>

### [191] [Objective-oriented quantitative investment: A specification-driven framework for automated synthesis of trading strategy pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10410)
*Liangliang Zhang*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出"面向目标的量化投资（OOQI）"框架，将策略流水线建模为带接口契约的可组合模块设计空间，将投资者意图形式化为可组合的策略档案规范语言，并通过编译器将规范翻译为约束装配并逐条款验证。在32个合成流水线装配的演示中，面向结果的选取得到了最高的样本内信息比率但仅满足25%规范，而面向规范的选取满足100%规范且仅付出5.5%的分数代价。理论证明满意度驱动的合成一般是NP难的，但在无冲突机制下可常数因子逼近；规范形成与装配对偶的格结构；每条条款携带拉格朗日影子价格；滚动重认证通过e-process实现随时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动量化研究的范式是"结果导向"的：所有系统都回答同一个问题——哪个策略在标量指标上得分最高？作者认为这个问题是不完整的，因为专业投资者真正需要的不只是最高收益，而是一种"身份"：纯选股alpha、不受风格暴露污染、在单边市场下跌中稳健、同时满足换手率和容量预算。现有范式无法表达和满足这些多维约束。

Method: 提出OOQI框架：(i)将策略流水线建模为带显式接口契约的可互换模块类型化设计空间（参考实例中有8.85×10^8种装配）；(ii)将投资者意图形式化为策略档案规范——一种可组合的、可测量且可证伪的条款语言，来自八个需求族，具有硬/软语义和交互代数；(iii)编译器将规范翻译为约束装配并逐条款验证。为防止大搜索空间导致满意度虚高，开发了验证协议：将满意度率本身视为统计对象，对搜索宽度、时间留出和随机装配零模型进行缩减。

Result: 合成演示中，32个流水线装配下，结果导向选取达到最高的样本内信息比率但仅满足25%的规范；规范驱动选取满足100%规范，代价仅为5.5%的分数降低。理论结果包括：满意度驱动合成一般NP难但无冲突机制下可常数因子逼近；规范构成与装配对偶的格；每条条款有拉格朗日影子价格；滚动再认证通过e-process实现随时有效。

Conclusion: 该论文挑战了量化研究中"标量指标最大化"的主导范式，主张用规范驱动的框架替代结果驱动的框架，以更忠实地表达和满足专业投资者的多维、可证伪的策略需求。理论分析和合成实验支持了这一框架的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Automated quantitative research has made striking progress, yet each system answers the same question: which strategy scores highest on a scalar metric? We argue this question is incomplete. Professional investors do not order "the highest return"; they order an identity--pure stock-selection alpha uncontaminated by style exposure, resilient in unilateral market declines, within turnover and capacity budgets. We call the incumbent paradigm result-oriented and propose Objective-Oriented Quantitative Investment (OOQI): a specification-driven framework in which (i) the full strategy pipeline is modeled as a typed design space of interchangeable modules with explicit interface contracts (8.85 x 10^8 assemblies in our reference instantiation); (ii) investor intent is formalized as a strategy profile specification--a composable language of measurable, falsifiable clauses from eight requirement families, with hard/soft semantics and an interaction algebra; and (iii) a compiler translates specifications into constrained assemblies and verifies satisfaction clause-by-clause. Because search over large assembly spaces inflates apparent satisfaction, we develop a verification protocol treating the satisfaction rate itself as a statistical object, subject to deflation for search width, temporal holdout, and random-assembly null models. A synthetic demonstration with 32 pipeline assemblies shows that result-oriented selection attains the top in-sample information ratio while satisfying only 25% of the specification, whereas specification-driven selection satisfies 100% of it at a 5.5% score cost. The accompanying theory shows satisfaction-driven synthesis is NP-hard in general yet constant-factor approximable in a conflict-free regime; specifications form a lattice dual to assemblies; each clause carries a Lagrangian shadow price; and rolling re-certification is anytime-valid via e-processes.

</details>

### [192] [When the Fed Speaks: Dynamics and Forecasts of the Volatility Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10693)
*Lukasz Adamski,Robert Slepaczuk*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦公开市场委员会（FOMC）预定会议日期前后隐含波动率（IV）曲面的演变。首先验证公告前IV是否上升，并检查该效应在短期、虚值期权及高波动率制度下是否更强；其次检验机器学习（ML）框架能否在预测该效应上超越随机游走基准，模型引入与FOMC会议日期相关的特征，采用二维卷积LSTM模型直接学习IV曲面的时空信号，无需降维。研究发现ML框架的优势受限于IV曲面的噪声特征，但在异常日期仍能有效预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究主要验证FOMC公告前的IV升高效应是否存在及强度，但缺乏对此效应的定量预测，且少有机器学习方法直接应用于IV曲面预测；此外，现有ML预测IV曲面的工作多依赖降维技术，忽略曲面本身的时空结构信息。

Method: 采用二维卷积LSTM模型直接处理IV曲面，无需维度约减，能够学习曲面中的时空信号；模型中加入与FOMC预定会议日期相关的外生特征，以捕捉公告前不确定性升高的效应；对比基准为随机游走模型。

Result: 实证证实IV在公告前上升，该效应在短期、虚值期权及高波动率制度下更强。ML框架的预测优势受限于IV曲面的噪声特征，但模型仍能在FOMC异常日期有效预测IV曲面。

Conclusion: ML模型能够有效预测IV曲面，包括在FOMC公告等异常日期场景下；但IV曲面固有的噪声特性限制了ML相对基准模型的优势，未来需考虑噪声抑制或更精细的特征工程。

Abstract: Our primary goal is to forecast and empirically examine the evolution of the implied volatility (IV) surface, with particular focus on the dates of scheduled meetings of the Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC). Firstly, we check if IV increases before the announcement and if thes effect is stronger for short-dated, out-the-money (OTM) options in high volatility regimes. In the second part, we turn the focus to verifying if the ML framework can beat the benchmark random walk in forecasting this effect. A feature related to dates of scheduled FOMC meetings augments the model, which allows us to discover if it can learn the effect of elevated pre-announcement uncertainty. Our contribution relies mainly on the quantitative prediction of the pre-announcement effect and the inclusion of exogenous information inside the ML framework used for the IV surface forecasting. It is also on of the first attempts to apply ML models directly on the IV surface without relying on dimensionality reduction. To achieve this, we employ a convolutional two-dimensional LSTM model, which is capable of learning spatio-temporal signals in the surface. Our analysis reveals that the edge of the ML framework can be limited due to the noisy characteristics of the IV surface. Nevertheless, our study reinforces the perspective that ML models can effectively forecast the IV surface also during abnormal days.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Closed-Loop LLM Co-Pilots for Digital Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09949)
*Serge Kernbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大语言模型（LLM）在复杂生物系统中的应用，从数据分析演进到自主AI引导实验。基于49通道植物传感器网络，系统实现了从人在环到完全自主控制的闭环架构，并在垂直农场和单株植物场景中验证。相比周期性控制，最小时间模式缩短35%生产周期，能量优化模式节省18%能耗，且AI自主发现了暗诱导叶绿素积累策略，实现67.9%节能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生物系统研究依赖数据分析与人工干预，缺乏自主性；大语言模型具备从数据处理到决策控制的能力，但尚未在复杂生物系统中实现真正的自主闭环实验。研究旨在将LLM从分析工具转变为自主实验执行者，提升数字农业的效率与可及性。

Method: 构建基于49通道植物传感器网络（多光谱、电化学、介电模态）的LLM驱动闭环框架。系统实时处理生物物理数据，LLM评估植物生理状态并触发硬件执行器以优化微气候、执行表型协议或诱导受控胁迫。通过自然语言界面同时服务专家与非专家用户。

Result: 在垂直农场和单株植物场景中完成三个案例验证。生产规模部署中，智能体执行多参数优化，平衡生物量积累、叶绿素含量和能耗。最小时间模式缩短生产周期35%；能量优化模式降低能耗18%且仅轻微增加培养时间；自主发现暗诱导叶绿素积累策略实现67.9%节能。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM转化为数字农业的自主副驾驶，建立了直接的AI-生物界面，显著改善了成本效益比，降低了计算资源和专家劳动的门槛，为复杂生物系统的数据驱动探索提供了新范式。

Abstract: This study evaluates the application of Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex biological systems, evolving from data analysis to autonomous, AI-guided experimentation. The framework is driven by data from a 49-channel phytosensor network, encompassing multispectral, electrochemical, and dielectric modalities. To enhance accessibility, the system provides real-time natural-language interpretation for both specialists and non-experts. However, its core advantage lies in the transition from human-in-the-loop analysis to autonomous control. Processing biophysical data, the LLM evaluates plant physiology and triggers hardware actuators to optimize microclimates, execute phenotyping protocols, or induce controlled stress scenarios. This closed-loop architecture establishes a direct AI-biology interface, enabling data-driven exploration of complex biosystems and ecologies. The framework was validated across three case studies, based on a vertical farm and a single-plant setup and deciphered complex micro- and macro-fluctuations in plant physiology. Agents in a production-scale deployment executed multi-parameter optimization, balancing biomass accumulation, chlorophyll content, and energy consumption. The LLM processed biosensing telemetry to modulate full-spectrum, 450 nm, and 660 nm lighting at 2-hour intervals. Compared to periodic control, the system in minimal-time mode reduced the production cycle by 35%. In the energy-optimization mode, it reduced energy consumption by 18% with only a marginal increase in cultivation time, exploiting physiological inertia via light pulses. Finally, the agents autonomously developed an unforeseen strategy of dark-induced chlorophyll accumulation, resulting in a 67.9% energy saving. This framework transforms LLMs into autonomous co-pilots for digital agriculture, improving the cost-to-value ratio and lowering computational and expert-labor constraints.

</details>


### [194] [SPOTting the Future: Lookahead Explanations for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09967)
*Tamar Gozlan,Claudia V. Goldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPOT是一种模型无关的、基于采样的深度强化学习策略解释框架，通过在环境模拟器中采样子动作和递归模拟后继状态来构建有限时域的可解释树，从而以经验方式表示策略的动作偏好及其下游演化。该方法在SUMO-RL交通信号控制领域得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习智能体在复杂环境中表现出色，但其决策过程难以解释。现有方法（如单时间步特征归因）无法揭示策略的时序行为与下游后果，需要一种能够全面理解策略偏好和演化路径的可解释性工具。

Method: SPOT框架通过访问策略和环境模拟器，采用采样方式构建有限时域树：采样动作并递归模拟后继状态，形成一棵可解释的树结构，从经验角度表示策略的动作选择偏好及其后续状态演化路径。同时提供形式化保证，证明其渐近恢复策略唯一最可能动作，并刻画高熵策略下的分歧行为。

Result: 在SUMO-RL交通信号控制领域的案例研究中，SPOT的树形表示成功用于检查策略偏好、比较多种备选未来轨迹，并揭示出单时间步特征归因方法无法捕捉的下游行为。

Conclusion: SPOT为DRL策略提供了一种新颖的可解释性框架，能够捕捉时序维度的策略行为，弥补了现有单时间步解释方法的不足，并在真实领域应用中展示了其诊断与洞察能力。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents achieve strong performance in complex environments, yet their decision-making processes remain difficult to interpret. We introduce SPOT (Sampling Policy Observation Tree), a novel model-agnostic, sampling-based framework for interpreting DRL policies. Given access to the policy and an environment simulator, SPOT constructs an interpretable finite-horizon tree by sampling actions and recursively simulating the resulting successor states. The tree provides an empirical representation of the policy's action preferences and their possible downstream evolution. We provide formal guarantees establishing SPOT's asymptotic recovery of the policy's unique most probable action and characterizing its disagreement behavior under high-entropy policies. We demonstrate SPOT in the SUMO-RL traffic-signal control domain. The case study illustrates how its tree-based representation can be used to inspect policy preferences, compare alternative future trajectories, and reveal downstream behaviors that are not visible through single-timestep feature-attribution methods.

</details>


### [195] [MIDAS: Mutual Information Disentanglement with Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09986)
*Yuhua Wen,Yingying Zhou,Qifei Li,Yingming Gao,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao,Ya Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MIDAS框架，通过互信息解耦与不确定性感知融合，有效处理多模态情感分析中的模态不完整问题，在三个数据集上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多假设模态完整可用，而真实场景常出现模态缺失或不完整；已有方法依赖数据补全和启发式协调约束，无法有效提取和利用不完整多模态数据中的任务相关信息。

Method: 采用变分建模策略，以多元高斯潜变量表示每个模态并将其分解为共享因子和独有因子；设计极小极大目标，最小化共享与独有空间之间的互信息以实现稳定解耦，同时最大化跨模态共享空间之间的互信息以增强语义对齐；引入不确定性感知融合机制，利用后验方差作为可靠性指示器自适应加权潜特征。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的数据集上进行大量实验，MIDAS在各种不完整设置下均取得稳定且一致的性能提升，优于多个竞争基线，验证了其对不完整数据场景的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MIDAS通过互信息解耦与不确定性感知融合的统一框架，有效解决了多模态不完整条件下的表征重构问题，为实际应用中的不完整多模态情感分析提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Most existing multimodal sentiment analysis approaches assume access to complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world applications frequently encounter incomplete or corrupted modalities, posing a critical challenge. Although several methods have been proposed to tackle this issue, they mainly rely on data imputation and heuristic coordination constraints, which fail to effectively extract and leverage task-relevant information from the incomplete multimodal data. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework termed Mutual Information Disentanglement with uncertainty-Aware fuSion (MIDAS), which effectively restructures multimodal representations under incomplete conditions. MIDAS adopts a variational modeling strategy to represent each modality with multivariate Gaussian latent variables and further decomposes them into shared and exclusive factors. To obtain reliable representations, we design a minimax objective that minimizes the mutual information between shared and exclusive spaces for stable disentanglement, while maximizing the mutual information among shared spaces across modalities to enhance semantic alignment. In addition, an uncertainty-aware fusion mechanism is introduced, where posterior variance is leveraged as a reliability indicator to adaptively weight latent features during fusion, ensuring robust integration even when modalities are incomplete. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that MIDAS achieves strong and consistent performance gains over competitive baselines across a wide range of incomplete settings, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness for incomplete data scenarios.

</details>


### [196] [Towards Sustainable Artificial Intelligence: A Comprehensive Review and Comparative Analysis of Deep Learning Models' Carbon Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09998)
*Samar Garrab,Sarra Boughriou,Manel BenSassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了绿色AI、绿色深度学习和优化技术的研究进展，比较了多种碳排放测量工具，并通过CPU实验评估了六种深度学习模型在多标签分类任务中的碳排放情况，发现训练阶段是主要排放来源，且模型复杂度与精度提升并非成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI和ML模型规模不断扩大，其高能耗和碳排放问题日益突出，尤其是大规模深度学习模型的部署带来了巨大的计算资源需求，亟需系统性地审视和减少AI模型的环境影响。

Method: 采用系统综述方法梳理绿色AI相关研究，比较多种碳测量工具；同时设计CPU实验环境，实现六种深度学习模型进行多标签分类任务，量化比较各模型碳排放总量并识别深度学习生命周期中各阶段的排放贡献。

Result: 训练阶段是碳排放的主要来源；模型架构复杂度增加并未系统性带来相应的精度提升，表明需要在预测性能和环境影响之间进行平衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了将可持续性考量纳入模型选择和AI系统设计的必要性，为绿色AI的发展提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) have become powerful tools for supporting and automating complex human tasks. Despite their benefits, growing attention has been directed toward their environmental implications, primarily due to their high energy demands and associated carbon emissions. This concern is particularly relevant in light of the increasing deployment of large-scale models, especially Deep Learning (DL) architectures, which provide advanced predictive capabilities but require substantial computational resources. This paper presents a systematic review of research on Green AI, Green DL, and optimization techniques aimed at reducing the environmental impact of AI models. In addition, we examine and compare several carbon measurement tools for estimating emissions generated by AI algorithms. To complement the review, we conducted an empirical evaluation using a CPU-based experimental setup, in which six DL models were implemented for a multi-label classification task. The objective was to quantify and compare their overall carbon emissions and to determine which stages of the DL lifecycle contribute most significantly to the total footprint. The results show that the training phase is the primary source of emissions. Moreover, the findings reveal that increased architectural complexity does not systematically translate into proportional accuracy gains, highlighting the importance of carefully balancing predictive performance and environmental cost. These results reinforce the need to integrate sustainability considerations into model selection and AI system design.

</details>


### [197] [ReCBM: Uncertainty-Gated Relational Reasoning for Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10004)
*An Sui,Yuzhu Li,Fuping Wu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReCBM是一个面向概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）的不确定性门控关系推理框架，通过在瓶颈中引入语义定义的概念关系（共现、蕴含、排除），并用不确定性来引导关系细化，从而提升在概念状态不可靠场景下的推理鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CBM变体虽然通过更丰富的概念表示、不确定性估计和依赖建模实现了改进，但在不可靠概念状态下的稳健推理仍未得到充分探索。误导性的语义证据会传播到瓶颈中，损害解释和下游预测。

Method: 提出ReCBM框架，包含三个关键设计：(1) 在瓶颈中引入语义定义的概念关系（共现、蕴含、排除）来规定概念间的证据交换方式；(2) 使用不确定性来指导关系细化；(3) 在证据交换过程中用不确定性调制每个概念的贡献度。支持不确定性感知的干预机制。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，ReCBM在概念缺失和概念翻转场景下提高了概念恢复和任务恢复能力，支持不确定性感知干预，并且能够提取紧凑的任务相关概念子集而不降低下游性能。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性门控的关系推理，ReCBM有效解决了CBM在不可靠概念状态下的鲁棒推理问题，在提高鲁棒性的同时保持了模型的可解释性和下游性能。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide an interpretable framework by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts, enabling semantic inspection and test-time intervention.
